Word Shuffle destroys all compositional structure and preserves only the token inventory, isolating lexical activation. The key intervention is n-gram shuffle: the rationale is segmented into contiguous token windows of size n, token order is preserved within each window, and the windows are randomly permuted. n=1 is effectively a bag of words; larger n gradually restores local structure. The recovery curve as n increases reveals how much useful probe-time signal is carried by short local neighborhoods.

The paper also runs several controls to rule out simpler explanations. These include explicit answer copying, option-anchor effects, generic reference text, and the possibility that any input text would show the same small-window recovery. A question-text perturbation control is especially important: the same phenomenon does not appear as strongly when perturbing the question itself, suggesting that CoT-style rationale text has a distinctive local structure that the model can exploit. The qualitative pattern is tested across multiple datasets, model families, parameter scales, and both open- and closed-source configurations.50:T53d,The most illustrative setup is Config D, where Qwen3.5-35B-A3B generates rationales and Qwen2.5-14B serves as the probe. This pairing yields a large observable CoT gain, making perturbation effects easier to measure. The headline numbers from the motivating example are already informative: input-output prompting gives 50.9% accuracy, full CoT reaches 83.9%, Sentence Shuffle remains at 83.1%, Word Shuffle still achieves 66.5%, and preserving only 2-token windows recovers performance to 76.5%. That means most of the gain beyond a word bag can be recovered with extremely short local spans.

Each condition pools three random seeds with 500 examples per seed, for n=1500 total, and significance is tested with two-sided McNemar tests. The paper reports that the same qualitative n-gram recovery pattern holds across multiple generator–probe configurations, model families, scales, and datasets, with the effective recovery window typically around 2–3 tokens. The control experiments show that the effect is not primarily explained by answer leakage, option repetition, or generic lexical overlap. Another useful result is that perturbing question text does not show the same strong small-window recovery, which supports the claim that the phenomenon is specific to rationale text rather than a generic property of LM input processing.51:T411,For a skim-level read, focus on the introduction, the main perturbation setup, and the control experiments. Start with the motivating table in the introduction: it already captures the paper’s core claim. Then read the experimental setup carefully to understand why the generator–probe split isolates probe-time behavior and how explicit answer leakage is removed. After that, inspect the main n-gram recovery results. The important object is the shape of the recovery curve, not any single score. Finally, read the controls on answer copying, option anchors, generic text, and question perturbations; these are what make the interpretation credible.

A 30–40 minute read is enough to extract the main contribution. If reading more deeply, two follow-up questions are worth keeping in mind: whether stronger reasoning-tuned models rely less on these local cues, and how this probe-time locality relates to pretraining-induced short-range statistics, attention locality, and the templated nature of rationale data in instruction tuning.52:T752,The core contribution is a double-gate evaluation protocol. For each compositional instance of depth k, the authors define the required atomic facts, multiple paraphrased probes for each atom, and aligned sub-questions that match how each atom appears inside the chain. An example passes the atomic gate only if every atomic fact is answered correctly and consistently across paraphrases. It then passes the sub-question gate only if every aligned sub-question is answered correctly in isolation. Residual composition failure is measured only on examples that pass both gates but still fail on the main compositional question.

This changes the estimand. Instead of attributing the entire single-hop to multi-hop drop to composition, the protocol conditions on stable atomic access and explicit local verbalization. The paper then decomposes post-training gains into three channels: Δatom for atomic stability, Δcomp for residual composition at matched atoms, and Δdepth for the depth at which residual failure first exceeds 50%. This decomposition is the main conceptual tool of the paper.

The benchmark is D4v2, a temporal compositional dataset with 390 main questions and 2,490 atomic sub-questions spanning four task families and depths 2 through 11. The evaluation covers six open-weight models in the 7B–13B range across several post-training recipes: base, RLHF, SFT reasoning-trace distillation, and native outcome-verified RL. A particularly strong part of the design is the controlled same-base LoRA study comparing SFT-answer, SFT-trace, and GRPO-style training, which helps isolate objective-level effects from base-model confounds. The authors also note an important caveat: residual composition failure is still an upper bound on pure composition failure, because formatting issues, distractor attention, and decoding-path degeneracy can also contribute.53:T636,There are four results worth retaining. First, base models are mostly filtered out by the atomic gate rather than by composition. The paper reports that base models admit fewer than five cases at any depth, which means many apparent multi-hop failures are really failures of stable atomic access.

Second, once atomic stability is matched within 2 percentage points, residual composition failure can still vary from 0% to 47% at depth 2 depending on the post-training recipe. This is the empirical signature of composition collapse: nearly identical atomic knowledge, sharply different composition behavior. Under matched data and compute, outcome-verified RL substantially outperforms SFT trace distillation on this metric.

Third, training on a specific composition depth closes much of the residual failure at that depth but transfers only weakly to nearby held-out depths. That suggests the learned behavior is not a broadly extrapolating compositional procedure.

Fourth, enabling chain-of-thought reduces residual failure by roughly 3–5×. This is an important clue about mechanism: a large part of the bottleneck lies in generation-time computation, not only in static knowledge representation. The model may already possess the required facts, yet still fail to assemble them without enough inference-time workspace.

Because the paper combines cross-model comparisons with same-base LoRA interventions, the results support a stronger claim than simple leaderboard differences: training objectives themselves can alter the composition channel after atomic knowledge is controlled.54:T4d1,This is labeled skim, but it deserves a careful skim if you care about reasoning evaluation or post-training analysis. Start with the introduction and focus on why the usual compositionality gap is an inadequate estimand. Then read the method section and Figure 1 closely; the double-gate protocol, residual composition failure, and the three-channel decomposition are the conceptual core. After that, inspect the main result tables with attention to comparisons made under matched atomic stability rather than raw overall accuracy. The same-base LoRA intervention is the next priority, because it most directly isolates the effect of the training objective. Finally, read the sections on CoT-enabled evaluation and depth transfer, which speak to two important questions: whether generation-time computation matters, and whether compositional improvements generalize across chain depth.

A focused 45-minute read should be enough for the main ideas. If going deeper, two follow-up questions are worth tracking: whether the protocol extends cleanly beyond temporal reasoning into broader domains, and how much of the measured residual failure is still due to decoding or formatting artifacts rather than internal compositional limits.55:T48e,The method centers on two diagnostic metrics. Structural Shortcut Reliance (SSR) uses attention heads to measure whether answer generation over-relies on a small set of salient shortcut cues—such as minimal query-to-answer paths in linearized graph triplets—while underusing the broader contextual evidence needed for verification. The input is partitioned into core structural cues and contextual cues, and SSR compares the attention mass assigned to each. Semantic Alignment Score (SAS) probes FFN representations to assess whether the model’s internal states stay aligned with the supporting evidence during generation. If semantic grounding weakens, the model is more likely to fall back to parametric memory and produce an answer that is plausible from training but unsupported by the current input. The paper studies these metrics on single-hop and multi-hop graph reasoning as well as tabular reasoning, and uses a quadrant-style analysis to show that attention bias and semantic drift do not always move together. The appendices reportedly include controls showing that the metrics are not reducible to shallow structural heuristics or lexical overlap.56:T465,The setup reads two quantities from the same frozen forward pass of an instruction-tuned decoder LM. At the last prompt-token position, the authors read residual-stream activations from a chosen layer; from the final state, they also read the model’s own lm_head probabilities for Yes and No. The probe is an L2-regularised logistic regression trained only on commonsense-aligned items and then frozen for all reported evaluations. Each prompt is paired with a counterfactual minimal pair that changes only the words needed to flip the answer direction, such as cause versus prevent or vaccinated versus not vaccinated. The same idea is also expressed as a causal-direction subspace: paired hidden-state differences from commonsense items are stacked, and the top-k right singular vectors define the readout directions. Two same-shape controls are used: a sham subspace fit on nonsense prompts and Haar-random directions. This makes the claim of encoding stricter than ordinary probing: the readout must recover the evidence-supported answer, flip on the counterfactual pair, and beat controls that should remain at chance.57:T69d,The environment is built on frozen GPT-2 small with 12 layers and 12 heads per layer, giving 144 discrete actions. Each action selects one attention head and zero-ablates its output at hook_z. Episodes last up to 50 steps, and repeated selection of the same head within an episode is masked out. Training uses two tasks only, induction and IOI. At every reset, the environment samples one of these tasks and regenerates a fresh batch, so the policy cannot simply memorize a fixed ranking of heads. The reward is the key design choice. For the target task, the paper measures the drop in a task-specific logit-difference metric after ablation. For a control batch of natural English text from WikiText, it measures the increase in cross-entropy. The final reward is the difference between these two effects. Heads that are broadly useful to the model should hurt both the target task and ordinary language modeling, yielding a smaller net reward; heads that are disproportionately important for the target task should receive larger positive reward. The observation includes a one-hot task indicator, a binary mask of previously tried heads, and a vector of normalized rewards for heads already tested in the current episode. This lets the policy adapt its search online within an episode. The policy itself is a small feedforward actor-critic trained with PPO over 8 parallel environments for 2e5 environment steps. At inference time, the authors also add a best-of-K wrapper: the policy samples K candidate actions, the environment scores them in parallel, and the highest-reward action is executed. Generalization is tested on a held-out docstring-completion task never seen during training.58:T46e,The main empirical claim is that a single PPO policy trained on induction and IOI can approach per-episode oracle performance on those tasks and also transfer to an unseen docstring-completion task. The abstract further states that the heads preferred by the learned policy overlap with known circuits identified by prior manual or semi-automated analyses, which is important because it suggests the policy is not merely exploiting noise. The key experimental setup is explicit: GPT-2 small, 144 actions, episode length 50, 8 parallel environments, 2×10^5 total environment steps, and evaluation with K in {1,5}. The most informative experiments are likely the reward ablations, comparisons against oracle and random baselines, training stability with vectorized PPO versus the collapsed single-environment variant, and transfer to the held-out docstring task. The provided text fragment does not include the full result tables, so exact gains should not be fabricated. The important point is that the paper is trying to demonstrate policy-level generalization in circuit discovery, not just recovery of known heads on one benchmark.59:T864,This is a perspective and framework paper rather than a single-method contribution. Its technical content has three main layers.

First, it formalizes LLM development decisions in the potential-outcomes framework. A system choice is treated as an intervention or treatment A, observed context as X, and the outcome of interest as Y. In this setup, A may represent a pretraining data mixture, prompt template, routing assignment, retrieval policy, judge configuration, or alignment strategy; Y may be perplexity, task success, preference score, safety metric, latency, or a combined utility. This turns common engineering questions into estimands such as E[Y(a)] or treatment effects.

Second, the paper maps this framing across the LLM pipeline: pretraining data decisions, alignment and preference collection, deployment-time routing, evaluation through arenas and LLM-as-a-judge, and agentic workflows. The recurring argument is that these settings rely heavily on observational logs rather than randomized data, and those logs are shaped by prior policies, annotator behavior, and evolving environments. As a result, naive comparisons can be badly confounded. The routing example is especially clear: if harder prompts are systematically sent to larger models, raw logged outcomes can make the larger model look worse.

Third, the paper discusses practical principles for applying causal inference in LLM settings, including identifiability, confounding control, overlap, robustness, and the use of modern causal ML tools in high-dimensional regimes. It does not present one unified estimator, but it does make a concrete case for why observational analysis is often insufficient and where causal methods can improve statistical validity and data efficiency.

From a pretraining perspective, the most useful move is the explicit treatment of data-mixture design as an intervention problem. That framing naturally leads to counterfactual questions: what would have happened if a domain had been excluded, or if its weight had changed, after accounting for interactions with tokenizer behavior, training budget, curriculum, and evaluation overlap?5a:T41e,This is best read with a concrete problem in mind rather than linearly as a general survey.

If your focus is pretraining, start with the introduction and the sections on pretraining data decisions. The key point is why data mixture changes should be treated as interventions rather than ordinary hyperparameter tuning.

If you care more about evaluation and alignment, focus on the sections on human preferences, LLM-as-a-judge, arenas, and routing. Those sections are the most actionable because they spell out specific sources of bias that often appear in practice.

A useful reading lens is to ask three questions in each section: what is the treatment, what is the context, and what is the outcome? Why can’t logged data be compared directly? And what identification assumption would be hardest to justify in practice? Reading it this way is more productive than treating it as a broad conceptual essay.

The main thread can be covered in about 20–30 minutes. Applying the ideas seriously would require additional background in causal inference.5b:T74d,The method, AKBE, explicitly probes the model’s knowledge boundary during training. For each question, it runs dual-path rollouts under the current policy: one path with tool access and one path without tools. The two outcomes are then compared to infer whether the question is inside the model’s current parametric knowledge boundary and, if tools are needed, what the minimum effective tool-use pattern looks like.

The paper defines four cases. If the with-tool path is correct and the no-tool path is wrong, the sample is Tool-dependent: the model genuinely needs tools. In this case, AKBE reinforces the correct trajectory with the minimum number of tool calls, not just any successful tool-using trajectory.

If both with-tool and no-tool paths are correct, the sample is Efficiency: tools were unnecessary. AKBE then reinforces the no-tool correct trajectory to suppress redundant calls.

If the with-tool path is wrong but the no-tool path is correct, the sample is Hallucination: tool use hurt performance, likely by injecting misleading evidence or derailing reasoning. AKBE again reinforces the no-tool correct trajectory.

If both paths are wrong, the sample is Both-wrong, and no extra boundary-guided supervision is applied beyond the base RL objective.

Two design choices matter. First, the supervision is on-policy, because the dual-path rollouts come from the current model. This lets the boundary estimate evolve with training rather than relying on static labels. Second, AKBE does not modify the reward function. Instead, it adds an auxiliary loss on top of standard agentic RL, which is how it avoids the usual reward-shaping failure mode. Relative to prior efficiency-oriented methods, the novelty is not merely reducing tool calls, but estimating, per instance, whether tools are needed at all and what the minimal necessary usage should be.5c:T564,The paper reports experiments on seven QA benchmarks with two backbone models, comparing standard agentic RL against AKBE and other efficiency-oriented baselines. The headline numbers are: +1.85 average accuracy improvement, 18% fewer tool calls, and 25% higher tool productivity. The authors emphasize that this is not an accuracy-efficiency tradeoff; in most settings, both improve together.

The more informative parts of the evaluation are the analyses. One is compatibility: AKBE can be plugged into multiple agentic RL algorithms rather than being tied to a single trainer. Another is training dynamics: the distribution of samples across Tool-dependent, Efficiency, and Hallucination categories changes over training, suggesting that the knowledge boundary is not static and is itself reshaped by RL. A third is category-level attribution: Efficiency supervision mainly removes redundant calls, Hallucination supervision reduces harmful tool reliance, and Tool-dependent supervision teaches more economical tool-use patterns when tools are genuinely needed.

From the available details, the paper is focused on training behavior rather than scale. It does not appear to hinge on very large models or massive data. The gains are meaningful from an engineering perspective, but they are not large enough to imply a new scaling law or a major shift in agentic RL fundamentals.5d:T4b3,Start with the introduction and the first motivating figure. The key claim is that tool calls steadily increase during RL training, and naive reward shaping creates the wrong optimization pressure. Then read the method section carefully, especially the dual-path rollout setup and the four-way categorization. That is the core of the paper.

In the experiments, prioritize three things: the main results table showing simultaneous gains in accuracy and efficiency; the training-dynamics plots showing how the boundary categories evolve; and the ablations or category analyses that isolate the role of each supervision type. For a quick pass, 20 to 30 minutes is enough if you focus on the method diagram, the main quantitative table, and one dynamic analysis figure.

Two points deserve closer scrutiny. First, dual-path rollouts add sampling cost. Check whether the paper quantifies the extra training overhead and whether the reduced tool usage compensates for it in practice. Second, the boundary estimate depends on current-policy rollouts and may be noisy. If the no-tool path is unstable, misclassification could inject noisy supervision. That issue likely determines how far the method can scale.5e:T6ef,The methodology is simple and fairly well controlled. The authors build HEAT-24, a deterministic synthetic benchmark with 24 tasks across six categories, all operating on the same git-backed workspace. The workspace contains 12 files, including YAML and JSON configs, Python code and tests, Markdown docs, a CSV file, and changelog fragments. Before each run, the repository is reset. Models modify files by emitting content inside a structured <<<WRITE:path>>> ... <<<END>>> marker, which the runner applies to the workspace. Verification is then done automatically using git diff, parsers, and test execution. This design avoids subjective LLM judging and keeps outcomes binary and auditable.

Harnesses come in three levels. Light includes only a role statement and the raw task instruction. Balanced adds a four-step process template and an allowed-file list. Strict adds a six-stage procedure, explicit success criteria, a verification specification, and recovery instructions. An important control detail is that the file-writing marker is present in the raw task instruction for all conditions, so the comparison is really about added process structure rather than access to the write protocol itself. The experiment crosses six models, four capability tiers, and three harness conditions for 432 total runs. Metrics are TSR and VTSR, which coincide here because verifier infrastructure does not fail. The paper also defines six automated failure labels: format_violation, wrong_answer, wrong_file, missing_change, unrelated_change, and tests_still_fail.

Methodologically, the contribution is not a new algorithm but a clean experimental frame for studying prompt-structure effects. It turns a vague prompt-engineering question into a reproducible intervention study.5f:T5da,The most striking result is the collapse of Gemini 2.5 Flash under more structured harnesses. With the light harness, VTSR is 95.8%. It drops to 58.3% under balanced and 66.7% under strict, decreases of 37.5 and 29.2 percentage points. The dominant failure mode is not task misunderstanding but format_violation: all 10 balanced failures and 8 of 8 strict failures are format-related. That suggests that for some strong chat-oriented models, extra harness structure can push the model toward over-compliance with procedural text while hurting actual execution fidelity.

The paper also reports the opposite pattern for the frontier reasoning model, which benefits the most from the strict harness. So the relationship is not “stronger models need less structure.” It is more like “different model types respond differently to structure.” Another useful result is that parameter count is a poor proxy. The authors note that a 2B model, Gemma4:e2B, matches the stability of a stronger open tier under harness variation, supporting their claim that instruction-tuning quality matters more than scale for this phenomenon.

There are clear limits. The authors themselves note that tier-level claims are exploratory because each tier is represented by only one model. And HEAT-24 is synthetic, deterministic, and tool-free beyond the workspace abstraction, so the findings should be read as controlled measurements of interface sensitivity rather than a full account of real-world agent behavior.15:["$","main",null,{"className":"flex-1 overflow-y-auto","children":["$","div",null,{"className":"mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 xl:max-w-6xl","children":["$","$L16",null,{"data":{"date":"2026-05-27","generated_at":"2026-05-30T18:41:19.142753+00:00","stats":{"total":149,"starred":1,"must_read":9,"skim":84,"glance":56,"by_category":{"llm_pretrain":4,"scaling":2,"architecture":12,"efficient":19,"eval":19,"multimodal":14,"multimodal_gen":12,"post_train":22,"mechanism":25,"coding_agent":3,"agent":17}},"categories":[{"id":"llm_pretrain","emoji":"🧠","name":"LLM 预训练","description":"文本LLM预训练、数据/Tokenizer、优化器、训练 recipe、训练稳定性、重磅技术报告（Scaling Law 归 scaling；不含 diffusion/flow/any-order 等建模范式）","papers":[{"id":"2605.26459","title":"MuCon: Clipped Muon Updates for LLM Training","abstract":"Muon-style optimizers take a matrix-valued momentum or preconditioned update $B = U \\operatorname{diag}(σ_1,\\ldots,σ_r) V^\\top$ and replace it with its canonical partial polar factor $\\operatorname{Pol}(B) = U V^\\top$. 是隐藏矩阵参数组的缩放参数化。其次看投影定理和低秩校正恒等式，它们决定了近似算法该怎么设计。然后看 polar/absolute-value formulation 和 rational Newton iteration，重点不是公式复杂度，而是它为什么只能作为 spectral filter，以及阈值特征值为什么难处理。Background 的 CompleteP/μP 部分建议细读到学习率和 studies a core optimizer primitive for Muon-style training. Standard polar Muon maps every nonzero singular value of a matrix update to one, which is a strong intervention. MuCon asks whether one can instead clip only the singular values that exceed a threshold, preserve the remaining magnitude information, and approximate this operation well enough for LLM training.","importance":"The relevance to pre-training is update spectrum control. Muon is attractive because it constrains matrix-valued hidden updates through an orthogonalized direction, but the polar step discards all singular-value magnitudes. MuCon changes the operation from full polar normalization to spectral clipping. This is closer to constraining update size than replacing the entire update geometry. The connection to SpectralP and CompleteP matters because the intended use case is hyperparameter transfer under simultaneous width and depth scaling: tune small, train larger, while keeping hidden matrix update spectra under control.","technical_approach":"$17","experiments":"The provided content is primarily mathematical and numerical-analysis oriented, and does not expose full training experiment tables. The motivation comes from CompleteP-style joint width-depth scaling, where controlled hidden-matrix update spectra are expected to help hyperparameter transfer. No verifiable model sizes, token counts, loss curves, or downstream metrics are available in the supplied text, so the paper should be treated as an optimizer-primitive and approximation report rather than a complete large-scale training recipe.","reading_guide":"Read carefully the early definitions that separate MClip_τ, MuCon, and SpectralP. MClip is the mathematical clipping map, MuCon is the optimizer primitive that inserts this map into the Muon pipeline, and SpectralP is the scaling parameterization for hidden matrix groups. Then focus on the projection theorem and the low-rank correction identity; these determine which numerical approximations are sensible. The polar/absolute-value formulation and rational Newton analysis are worth reading for their failure modes: the threshold is nonsmooth, and the Newton iteration is only a spectral filter. The CompleteP/μP background is useful because it explains why spectral update control is tied to width-depth hyperparameter transfer. This term is calculated fr","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-26T10:56:20+00:00","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2605_26842.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文要解决的问题是：在 Muon 已经通过矩阵正交化改善大模型训练效率的前提下，进一步引入对曲率和尖锐极小值更敏感的更新信号，避免优化过程只依赖一阶动量方向。","importance":"这篇工作对预训练研究的价值主要在优化器设计。Muon 已经开始进入大规模 MoE 训练实践，MONA 试图回答一个实际问题：能否在不破坏 Muon 谱范数约束和 Newton-Schulz 正交化流程的情况下，加入类似 ALTO 的梯度差分加速项。贡献量级偏工程增量，但方向有用，因为它直接作用于大规模预训练的收敛速度、稳定性和下游能力。","technical_approach":"MONA 的核心做法是把梯度差分的指数滑动平均作为加速项，先加到原始梯度中，再进入 Muon 的动量和正交化流程。梯度差分 g_k - g_{k-1} 可近似为 Hessian 乘以参数位移，因此包含局部曲率信息。论文强调这个加速项必须放在正交化之前。如果放在正交化之后，会破坏 Muon 依赖的矩阵几何结构；如果只作为外部 adaptor，也会和 Muon 的非线性 Newton-Schulz 变换产生不清晰的耦合。对矩阵参数，MONA 沿用 Muon 的矩阵级更新：动量缓冲、Newton-Schulz 近似极分解、谱范数约束和 RMS 缩放。对 embedding、bias 等向量参数，则回退到 AdamW。理论部分在非凸光滑假设和有界方差随机梯度假设下给出收敛分析，并讨论加速项帮助离开尖锐区域的机制。","experiments":"实验覆盖三个 MoE 规模：MOE-1B-A0d2B、MOE-6B-A0d5B 和 MOE-68B-A3B。最大模型训练到 1T tokens。论文称 MONA 在预训练收敛上优于 Muon 和 AdamW，并在 MOE-68B-A3B 上继续做 SFT，评测通用能力、数学推理和代码生成，结果保持优势。给出的正文片段没有完整列出各 benchmark 的具体分数和提升幅度，因此精读时应重点核对预训练 loss 曲线、token efficiency、不同规模下的稳定性，以及 SFT 后能力是否来自更低的预训练 loss，还是来自优化器改变了表示质量。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，预计 20-30 分钟。建议先看 Algorithm 1，确认加速项和 Muon 正交化的插入位置；再看理论部分的假设是否和真实大规模训练相差过大；最后看三个 MoE 规模的 loss 曲线和下游表格。需要特别留意两点：一是 MONA 相比 Muon 是否引入了额外超参敏感性，例如加速系数 alpha 和记忆系数 beta_a；二是实验是否控制了学习率、batch size、warmup、cooldown 和 weight decay，否则优化器对比容易受到 recipe 差异影响。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a practical optimizer question: given Muon’s matrix orthogonalization benefits for large-scale language model training, can one add curvature-sensitive acceleration without disrupting Muon’s spectral-norm update geometry?","importance":"The relevance is mainly in pretraining optimization. Muon has become a serious alternative to AdamW in large MoE training, and MONA explores whether gradient-difference acceleration can improve convergence and downstream quality while keeping Muon’s core update structure. The contribution is incremental but practically aligned with current large-scale training needs: better token efficiency, fewer sharp-minimum failures, and stable behavior under large-batch regimes.","technical_approach":"MONA adds an acceleration term computed from an exponential moving average of gradient differences. Since g_k - g_{k-1} approximates H_k times the parameter displacement, it provides a cheap proxy for local curvature. The key design choice is to inject this term before Muon’s momentum accumulation and Newton-Schulz orthogonalization. Injecting it after orthogonalization would break the orthogonal update structure; treating it as a generic external adaptor would leave its interaction with Muon’s nonlinear polar-decomposition approximation unclear. Matrix parameters follow the Muon-style pipeline with momentum, Newton-Schulz orthogonalization, spectral-norm-like control, and RMS scaling. Vector parameters such as embeddings and biases fall back to AdamW. The theory analyzes convergence under standard non-convex smoothness and bounded stochastic-gradient variance assumptions, and interprets the acceleration term as a mechanism for moving away from sharp regions.","experiments":"The empirical study covers three MoE settings: MOE-1B-A0d2B, MOE-6B-A0d5B, and MOE-68B-A3B. In this work, we analyze late-stage pre-training trajectories and uncover a \\textbf{Rank-1 Subspace} phenomenon: while raw optimization steps oscillate violently, consecutive \\emph{merged} checkpoints collapse onto a stable, approximately one-dimensional linear manifold. We theoretically ground this observation in a \\emph{river-valley} landscape ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-26T02:48:34+00:00","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2605_26484.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究晚期预训练 checkpoint averaging 为什么有效，以及能否在不做额外梯度更新的情况下继续利用训练轨迹。核心问题不是提出新的预训练 recipe，而是解释 PMA/LAWA 这类 late-stage model averaging 在 LLM 预训练末段产生收益的几何原因。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在训练末段和 checkpoint 利用。论文给出一个有用观察：原始 checkpoint 轨迹看起来振荡很大，但连续合并后的 checkpoint 会落到近似一维的线性子空间上。这个现象如果稳定成立，可以影响 cooldown、checkpoint averaging、训练末段外推、以及低成本模型选择。贡献量级偏机制分析和轻量后处理，不是新的大规模训练范式。","technical_approach":"方法先把 LAWA/PMA 形式化为对最近 n 个 checkpoint 的加权平均，并验证 uniform averaging 比 EMA 更适合作为基线。核心分析有两部分。第一，做相邻 checkpoint 的线性插值：原始 checkpoint 之间常出现中点 loss 更低的 convex-basin profile，说明 raw trajectory 带有较强高曲率振荡；而合并 checkpoint 之间的插值更接近单调下降，说明 averaging 把轨迹变平滑。第二，对合并轨迹做 PCA，发现第一主成分解释绝大部分方差，形成 Rank-1 Subspace。Extra-Merge 利用这个一维方向估计切向量，然后沿该方向外推，相当于把 averaging 看成几何低通滤波，再在滤出的主下降方向上走一步。理论部分用 river-valley landscape 解释：横向高曲率噪声被平均抑制，纵向低曲率下降方向被保留。","experiments":"实验覆盖 GPT-2/LLaMA 系列，从 124M 到 2B，并报告在 Pythia-12B 下游 zero-shot benchmark 上也有一致增益。文中明确给出的关键数字是：合并轨迹的第一主成分解释超过 94% 的方差；原始轨迹的方差分散在多个主成分上，且投影不单调。论文还比较了 uniform averaging 与 EMA，结论与 PMA 一致：uniform averaging 的验证 loss 更低、更稳定。优化器方面，作者声称 Extra-Merge 不只适用于 AdamW，也能迁移到 Muon 的 orthogonal update regime。给定材料中没有完整列出各模型的 loss 降幅和 benchmark checkpoint averaging helps during LLM pre-training, and whether the averaged trajectory can be further exploited without additional gradient steps. The main focus is the geometry of PMA/LAWA-style model averaging rather than a new pre-training objective or optimizer.","importance":"The useful signal for pre-training is in late-stage training dynamics. The paper reports that raw checkpoints oscillate substantially, while consecutive averaged checkpoints collapse onto an approximately one-dimensional linear manifold. If this phenomenon is robust, it can inform cooldown design, checkpoint averaging, post-hoc checkpoint extrapolation, and cheap final-model selection. The contribution is mainly mechanistic and post-processing-oriented, not a new large-scale training paradigm.","technical_approach":"The paper first frames LAWA/PMA as weighted averaging over the last n checkpoints sampled at interval τ, and uses uniform averaging as the main baseline after comparing it with EMA. The key analysis has two parts. First, it studies linear interpolation between adjacent checkpoints. Raw checkpoints often show a convex-basin profile, where the midpoint has lower validation loss than both endpoints, indicating high-curvature oscillation. In contrast, adjacent averaged checkpoints produce a much smoother and nearly monotone loss path. Second, PCA on the averaged trajectory shows that most variance is captured by the first component, forming a Rank-1 Subspace. Extra-Merge estimates the tangent of this subspace and extrapolates along it. The theoretical story uses a river-valley landscape: averaging suppresses transverse high-curvature noise while preserving the slow descent direction along the valley.","experiments":"The experiments cover GPT-2/LLaMA-style models from 124M to 2B, and the paper also reports consistent zero-shot gains on Pythia-12B downstream benchmarks. Specific impacts on pretraining research: (1) First unified functional form that simultaneously models multiple scaling dimensions and their interactions, enabling joint optimization of parameters, data, training steps, and hyperparameters within a single framework rather than fitting separate scaling curves; (2) Capable of modeling non-monotonic scaling behaviors (e.g., performance degradation after optimal training steps due to overfitting), a capability absent from all prior scaling laws; (3) Higher extrapolation accuracy means more reliable predictions before large model training, with practical value for training budget allocation and architecture selection; (4) The functional form applies to both upstream (pretraining validation) and downstream tasks, allowing prediction of both pretraining loss and downstream task performance in the same framework.","technical_approach":"$19","experiments":"The paper validates UNSL across multiple architectures and task sets. Because this source state is already computed during ordinary decoding, LRT adds a cross-layer recurrent latent pathway across positions without inserting pause tokens or extra depth loops, and the standard attention mechanism and KV-cache interface are preserved. To pretrain this ","starred":false,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-26T10:10:26+00:00","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2605_26797.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是一个架构与训练耦合问题：如何给自回归 Transformer 加入 token 级递归计算，让当前位置能复用前一位置的高层隐藏状态，同时不增加思考 token、不做额外深度循环，也不破坏标准 KV-cache 推理接口。","importance":"这篇工作值得精读，因为它把“增加测试时计算”和“保持预训练并行性”之间的矛盾处理得比较克制。很多递归或 latent reasoning 方法通过额外 token、重复 block 或分段记忆实现更多计算，但推理成本和训练形式都会改变。LRT 的选择更贴近现有 LLM 训练栈：只增加一条跨位置、跨层的 latent memory pathway，默认变体参数增加约 0.3%，解码仍是一 token 一次正常 forward，KV-cache 形状不变。对预训练研究的影响在于，它给出了一种低侵入式递归架构，并配套提出可并行训练的近似方案。这个组合比单纯提出 recurrence 更关键。","technical_approach":"LRT 选择某个源层 l_src，将位置 t 的隐藏状态 h_t^{l_src} 作为 recurrent memory m_t。生成位置 t 时，模型使用标准 causal attention 和 KV cache，同时把 m_{t-1} 注入当前 token 的计算。这样，当前位置的早期或中间层可以直接访问前一 token 的高层表示，而标准 Transformer 在 KV-cache 解码时，层 l 只能访问过去 token 同一层的缓存。论文研究了两类注入方式。KV Projection 把 memory 通过 attention 的 key-value 路径注入，使注意力计算能看到这条递归信息；Residual Injection 则把 memory 作为残差式信号注入层表示。默认方案把两者结合，并使用共享投影来控制参数量。训练难点在于，真实推理时存在 m_1 到 m_T 的顺序递归链，直接按 token 展开会失去 Transformer 预训练的并行优势。为此论文提出 interleaved parallel training：先用一次全序列初始化 forward 建立 memory buffer；然后把位置划分为若干互不重叠的交错子集，每个子集内部并行 refinement，并把更新后的状态写回 buffer；后续子集可以读取前面子集更新过的 memory。它不是精确 unroll，而是在固定训练预算下让每个 token 至少经历一次带递归记忆的 refinement，比 chunked recurrence 只在块边界传递 memory 更接近 token-level recurrence。","experiments":"实验使用 baseline-equivalent training compute，对比标准 Transformer 和 LRT 在不同模型规模、不同 tokens-per-parameter 预算下的 scaling behavior。主要指标包括 BPB，以及 Li et al. 2024 使用的 CORE。结果显示，LRT 的 scaling curve 向更低 BPB 和更高 CORE 移动，说明同等训练计算下质量更好。默认 shared-projection 变体只增加约 0.3% 参数。消融实验给出几个有用结论：KV Projection 和 Residual Injection 是互补的，组合优于单独使用；源层不是越靠后越好，上中层作为 recurrent memory 往往强于最终层，说明最终层可能过度贴近 logits 预测，而不一定是最适合作为下一 token 早期计算输入的通用状态；interleaved parallel training 比简单 chunked 训练更贴近推理时的 token-level recurrence。正文片段没有给出完整规模表和具体 BPB/CORE 数字，精读时应核对每个规模的 compute matching、训练 token 数、参数增量，以及 inference latency 是否只增加投影和注入开销。","reading_guide":"建议精读。先看 architecture 部分，重点是 m_t = h_t^{l_src} 的定义、memory 注入到哪些层、KV Projection 与 Residual Injection 的具体公式，以及为什么这条路径是标准 causal attention 没有的跨层信息通道。然后看 interleaved parallel training，特别是初始化 forward、交错子集划分、buffer 写回顺序，以及它和真实 sequential recurrence 的偏差。实验部分优先看 scaling curves，而不是只看单点 benchmark；再看源层选择、注入方式、共享/非共享投影、训练策略的消融。值得深挖的问题有三个：第一，训练时的 interleaved 近似和推理时严格递归之间是否存在分布偏移；第二，memory 只取 t-1 是否足够，还是需要更长 latent state；第三，这条高层状态反馈是否会改变深层表示的 specialization，进而影响长上下文和推理任务。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies how to add token-level recurrence to an autoregressive Transformer by reusing a high-level hidden state from the previous position, while preserving one-forward-pass decoding, standard causal attention, and the existing KV-cache interface.","importance":"This is a strong pretraining-architecture paper because it treats recurrence as a systems-compatible modeling change rather than as an add-on that requires extra thinking tokens or repeated block applications. Many recurrent or latent-computation methods increase inference cost by adding depth loops or additional decoding steps. LRT instead introduces a cross-position, cross-layer latent pathway with about 0.3% extra parameters in the default shared-projection variant. The more important contribution is the pairing of this architecture with a parallel training approximation, since exact token-by-token recurrence would remove the main efficiency advantage of Transformer pretraining.","technical_approach":"$1a","experiments":"The experiments compare LRT with matched-depth Transformer baselines under baseline-equivalent training compute, across model sizes and tokens-per-parameter budgets. The main reported outcomes are lower BPB and higher CORE, shifting the scaling curves in favor of LRT. The default shared-projection variant adds only about 0.3% parameters. Ablations indicate that KV Projection and Residual Injection are complementary, that an upper-middle source layer can outperform the final layer as recurrent memory, and that the training approximation matters. The source-layer result is especially informative: the final layer may be too specialized for next-token logits, while an upper-middle layer provides a more reusable latent state for the next position. The provided text does not include the complete numeric tables, so the key checks are compute matching, exact model sizes, token budgets, BPB/CORE deltas, and the measured inference overhead beyond the unchanged KV-cache shape.","reading_guide":"Read it closely. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-26T11:56:49+00:00","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2605_26895.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","stat.ML","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究的是一个长期被当作“实现细节”的部件：LLM backbone，只通过重复和排列 token，让因果模型在局部块内获得近似双向条件。这对 RWKV、Mamba、RetNet、GLA、Hyena 等 forward-state 模型都有潜在参考价值。局限也清楚：训练样本物理长度变为 3 倍，双向性只在 block 内成立，质量证据主要来自 7B 级模型和有限任务套件。","technical_approach":"核心方法是 triplet-block layout。每个逻辑生成块 g 被展开成三个连续物理块：b1 是带 mask 的副本，b2 是相同 mask 图案的副本并在其中计算 denoising loss，b3 是干净 ground-truth 副本，用来刷新递推状态后进入下一个块。对于 b2 中某个 masked 位置，模型已经读过完整的 b1，因此 hidden state 中包含该逻辑块所有未 mask token；同时，b2 内该位置左侧的未 mask token 也能通过普通因果路径进入预测。两条信息合起来，使严格从左到右的模型在块内获得伪双向条件。训练目标是在 b2 的 masked supervised positions 上做交叉熵，并借鉴 LLaDA-2.0 的 Confidence-Aware Parallel training：对已经 top-1 预测正确的位置加入熵压低项，让推理时的 threshold sampler 可以一次提交更多 token。推理使用 block-wise iterative denoising sampler，在一个块内多轮填充。","experiments":"实验发布了 B3D-RWKV-7.2B，基于 RWKV-7 规模的 7.2B 模型。作者在 8 个任务组成的套件上与因果语言模型比较，报告其准确率与 accuracy 表、throughput 表，以及是否有 block size、mask ratio、entropy regularization 的消融。Appendix A 值得扫一遍，主要确认训练和推理时物理序列长度、mask 构造、阈值提交策略的实现细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a concrete architectural mismatch: discrete diffusion LMs benefit from bidirectional conditioning and parallel denoising, while RWKV-style recurrent LMs are strictly causal and process tokens left to right. The goal is to combine diffusion-style generation with linear-time recurrent inference without rewriting the backbone.","importance":"The work is relevant to language-model architecture and alternative modeling objectives. Its main idea is to change the token layout rather than the model architecture, allowing a causal forward-state model to emulate block-level bidirectional conditioning. This is a useful design pattern for RWKV, Mamba-like state-space models, RetNet, GLA, and Hyena-style models. The trade-off is explicit: the physical training sequence is tripled, bidirectionality is local to a block, and the empirical evidence is centered on one 7B-scale RWKV variant and an eight-task evaluation suite.","technical_approach":"The method, called triplet-block layout, expands each logical block into three consecutive physical blocks. The first block is a masked copy, the second is the same masked copy where the denoising loss is applied, and the third is the clean ground-truth block used to refresh the recurrent state before the next logical block. For a masked position in the second copy, the model has already consumed the entire first copy, so its recurrent state contains all unmasked tokens in the block. It also sees the left-side unmasked tokens in the second copy through the ordinary causal path. Together, these two streams give pseudo-bidirectional access while preserving a strictly causal computation graph. Training uses cross-entropy on masked supervised positions and adds a confidence-aware entropy-sharpening term on positions whose current top-1 prediction is already correct. Inference performs block-wise iterative denoising with a threshold-based commit rule.","experiments":"The released model is B3D-RWKV-7.2B, trained around the RWKV-7 backbone scale. On an eight-task suite, it is reported to match the RWKV-7 baseline closely in accuracy while achieving an average 1.6x decoding-throughput improvement at comparable generation lengths. The key reported quantities are the 7.2B model size, the eight-task comparison, and the 1.6x throughput gain. The main cost is that training examples become three times longer under the triplet layout, so the efficiency argument is primarily about inference-time parallel token commitment combined with RWKV's linear-time recurrence.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25 to 35 minutes. Read Figure 1 and Section 3.1 carefully; they contain the core argument for pseudo-bidirectional conditioning in a causal model. Appendix A is useful for implementation details such as physical sequence construction, masking, and threshold scheduling.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这类工作值得看，但先别急着信速度数字；重点看依赖重排是否真的保留 diffusion 质量，以及 baseline 是否按采样步数和并行度对齐。","en":"Worth reading, but do not trust the speedup headline too quickly; check whether the dependency rewrite preserves diffusion quality and whether baselines are matched on sampling steps and parallelism."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Ke Lin","Yiyang Luo","Zhaolong Su","Yunya Song","Anyi Rao"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["diffusion-lm","rwkv","linear-time","bidirectional-modeling","generation-architecture"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25969","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.25969","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.27476","title":"Balancing Fidelity and Diversity in Diffusion Models via Symmetric Attention Decomposition: Hopfield Perspective","abstract":"We characterize the pre-softmax attention matrix $\\mathbf{QK^\\top}$ in transformers as an associative memory matrix encoding pairwise associations between input features. The central question is how to detect when attention settles into such metastable mixtures and how to intervene at inference time to trade off fidelity and diversity.","importance":"Its direct impact on LLM pretraining is limited, since the experiments target image diffusion models rather than language-model training. The useful part is conceptual. The paper treats the pre-softmax attention matrix QK^T as a dynamic associative memory, instead of only viewing attention as token-wise retrieval. This is relevant for researchers interested in Transformer dynamics, diffusion language models, masked modeling, and energy-based interpretations of generation.","technical_approach":"The main idea is to decompose QK^T into symmetric and skew-symmetric components. The symmetric component defines a Hopfield-style energy landscape and controls retrieval stability. The skew-symmetric component induces circulation, acting as a directional drift that can perturb metastable mixtures. Starting from Q=XW_Q and K=XW_K, the paper rewrites QK^T as a weighted superposition of feature outer products, then defines local fields, row-wise normalization, and a Hopfield retrieval operator. Standard self-attention appears as the special case where the normalization is row-wise softmax. The derived stability measures are used to diagnose retrieval states, while the skew component becomes an inference-time control knob.","experiments":"The provided text does not include the full quantitative results. From the abstract and introduction, the experiments claim that Hopfield-style stability measures correlate with the fidelity-diversity trade-off, and that modulating the skew-symmetric circulation term can change generation behavior. The key evidence to check is whether these metrics predict semantic mixing or structural errors, and whether the proposed intervention reduces feature leakage without simply collapsing diversity. Model scale, datasets, metrics, and effect sizes are not available in the supplied content.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Token-Adaptive Mixing of Activations","abstract":"Feedforward network (FFN) layers account for a large fraction of parameters and nonlinear expressivity in Transformer-based large language models (LLMs). Despite the evolution from ReLU and GELU to gated variants such as SwiGLU, most FFN designs still use a single fixed activation function, applying the same nonlinear transformation to all tokens. D-LLMs expose a trajectory of hidden states, and that trajectory contains information unavailable in one-shot or final-state monitoring. This makes the paper useful for researchers studying diffusion, masked, or any-order language models: the denoising process can support uncertainty estimation, verifier routing, and safety instrumentation. The work is not about training better D-LLMs, but it clarifies an interface that future training and post-training methods may exploit.","technical_approach":"The paper starts with lightweight probes over intermediate hidden representations of D-LLMs. It then studies probe margins, where a small margin means the representation lies close to the safety decision boundary. The key observation is safety hesitation: across denoising steps, some trajectories repeatedly fall near that boundary. Samples with more hesitation steps are harder for simple probes to classify correctly. D^2-Monitor uses this signal in a two-level design. A base probe runs continuously and provides both a first safety prediction and a hesitation estimate. A router compares the hesitation level with a threshold. If the sample appears difficult, an advanced probe is activated for second-stage classification; this advanced probe is trained on hesitation-heavy trajectories. The design trades fixed-cost monitoring for adaptive monitoring: easy samples stay cheap, while ambiguous or adversarial-looking samples receive more compute.","experiments":"The evaluation uses WildGuardMix, ToxicChat, and OpenAI-Moderation across four diffusion LLMs, with both intra-dataset and cross-dataset tests. The most important empirical result is the link between hesitation severity and probe failure: it supports the claim that denoising trajectories carry a useful difficulty signal, rather than merely providing extra representations to average over.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. In this paper, we show that selectively looping the early-middle transformer layers significantly improves both training efficiency and model performance in MDMs. We call this approach LoopMDM(Looped Masked Diffusion Model), which brings two key benefits: looping layers at training-time yields ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T17:58:24+00:00","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2605_26106.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是“MDM 能不能做语言建模”，而是更具体的一层：在 masked diffusion language model 已经靠多步去噪做迭代计算的前提下，Transformer 内部是否还值得再做 looped computation，以及怎样 loop 才能在固定训练算力下带来真实收益。难点在于，MDM 天生已经有时间维上的反复 refinement，如果再把层内计算重复一遍，收益可能只是表面上的“更深了”，也可能被训练 FLOPs 吃掉。作者关心的是一个很工程、也很基础的问题：如何把参数、深度、训练 FLOPs、推理 FLOPs 这几条轴重新解耦，让 MDM 获得比单纯堆层数更好的 compute allocation。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有三点。第一，它把 looped transformer 从 AR 语境带到了 masked diffusion LM，并且不是把它当作单纯参数共享技巧，而是当作训练效率和 test-time compute scaling 的手段来分析。第二，它给出一个比较清楚的经验结论：loop 不是越均匀越好，收益主要集中在 early-middle layers，说明 MDM 的表示加工存在明显的层位分工，这对后续做架构搜索、深度分配、adaptive compute 都有参考价值。第三，它提供了一个对“重复共享计算为何有效”的机制线索：收益和 masked positions 之间的交互增强有关，尤其集中在中等噪声区间。这一点比单纯报 benchmark 分数更有启发，因为它在解释 MDM 为什么需要额外迭代，而不是只说它有效。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者提出 LoopMDM：在 MDM 的 denoising transformer 中，不对所有层统一重复，而是选择少数 early-middle transformer layers 做 selective looping。核心设计有两个目标。其一，训练时通过重复应用共享层，在不增加参数的情况下制造 depth-scaling effect；其二，推理时允许改变 loop 次数，从而形成灵活的 compute scaling。\n\n从全文描述看，作者系统比较了几种 loop 方式：全层均匀 loop、不同层段 loop、不同 loop 次数配置。结论是，少量 early-middle layers 的 selective looping 已经能拿到大部分收益，而 uniform looping 的训练成本更高、性价比更差。这个结果说明，loop 的价值不只是“把网络变深”，而是让某些关键表示阶段反复做同一种变换，类似在固定参数预算下增加局部迭代求解能力。\n\n论文还进一步分析了 loop 与 diffusion timestep 的关系。作者发现 loop 带来的收益主要出现在 intermediate noise levels，而不是最干净或最脏的状态。因此他们提出一个更简单的 adaptive strategy：把更多 loop 分配给最有效的 timestep，减少其他 timestep 的重复计算，在性能基本保留的情况下继续降算力。这个设计很像把 compute budget 从“每步平均花”改成“在最需要 refinement 的地方多花”。\n\n机制分析部分也比较关键。作者通过受限生成顺序的 Sudoku 实验、attention 分析和 mask-to-mask interaction 统计，说明 looping 更像是在帮助模型处理被 mask 位置之间的全局约束传播，而不只是提升局部 token 分类器容量。对于 MDM 这类条件独立地预测 masked positions 的模型，这个观察很重要，因为它触及了这类模型最常被质疑的地方：如何建模跨 mask 的耦合关系。","experiments":"实验覆盖三个预训练语料：FineWeb-Edu、OpenWebText 和 LM1B。最核心的结果有两类。\n\n第一类是训练效率。在这三个语料上，LoopMDM 在 test NLL 上可以匹配同参数规模的普通 MDM，同时训练 FLOPs 最多减少 3.3 倍。若按总训练 FLOPs 对齐，LoopMDM 在 zero-shot perplexity、generative perplexity 和下游任务准确率上都稳定优于 baseline。这说明它不是单纯靠“更便宜地达到同样 NLL”，而是在相同算力预算下也能把预算转成更好的泛化表现。\n\n第二类是能力收益。文中点名 GSM8K 上相对同规模非 loop MDM 提升 +8.5%。更关键的是，它还优于那些 per-step training FLOPs 与 LoopMDM 匹配的更深 MDM。这个对比很重要，因为它排除了“只是因为更深”这一解释，支持 shared repeated computation 本身带来额外好处。\n\n消融方面，作者重点做了：1）loop 放在哪些层最有效；2）loop 次数变化的影响；3）与更深非共享模型的对比；4）不同噪声 timestep 上收益分布；5）Sudoku 这类全局约束任务上的行为差异。机制实验显示，随着 loop 次数增加，mask-to-mask attention interaction 增强；收益主要集中在中间噪声水平；浅层 baseline 无法解决的 Sudoku 约束传播问题，looped 版本可以解决。这些结果共同支持“looping 在做 iterative relational refinement”这一解释。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但有几个部分值得认真看。\n\n第一，先看引言和方法总览图，重点不是记住架构细节，而是理解作者到底在比较什么：参数量固定、训练 FLOPs 固定、per-step FLOPs 固定，这几个对齐方式结论不同，不能混着看。\n\n第二，重点看 selective looping 的消融表和图。要看清楚 early-middle layers 为什么比 uniform looping 更划算，这部分直接关系到后续能否迁移到更大模型。\n\n第三，GSM8K 与“更深但不 loop”的对比值得看，因为这是论文最有说服力的证据之一。它回答了 loop 是否只是深度替代品。\n\n第四，机制分析部分建议至少看 Sudoku 实验、attention 分析和不同 noise level 分钟能抓住主线。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a precise architectural question for masked diffusion language models: given that MDMs already perform iterative computation across denoising steps, is there still value in adding repeated computation inside the transformer itself, and if so, where should that repetition be placed? The challenge is that looping could easily collapse into a costly way of making the network effectively deeper, without delivering real gains at fixed training compute. serving。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies why low-bit KV-cache quantization hurts chunk-wise autoregressive video diffusion. The main issue is not just random quantization error. Quantized cached keys systematically attract too much attention mass, stealing probability from the current, unquantized chunk.","importance":"The work is only indirectly related to LLM pretraining, but it is useful for long-context generative serving and multimodal transformer systems. Its main value is a sharper diagnosis of KV-cache quantization: key noise changes the softmax partition function through the exponential, so preserving unbiased pre-softmax scores is not enough. This is a relevant lesson for KV compression in LLMs and VLMs as well.","technical_approach":"The proposed fix targets what the authors call Jensen bias. Quantizing cached keys introduces approximately zero-mean perturbations to attention scores. Because softmax applies an exponential, positive score perturbations are amplified more than negative perturbations are suppressed, so E[exp(s+η)] exceeds exp(s). Since only past cached keys are quantized while current-chunk keys remain full precision, the bias selectively inflates the cached-token contribution. The method estimates a per-score correction from the quantization step sizes available at inference time and subtracts it from cached-key attention logits before softmax. A second-order Taylor approximation gives a practical correction close to half of the score-noise variance. The procedure is training-free, leaves cached values unchanged, and is compatible with Hadamard rotation and token/group-wise quantization.","experiments":"The empirical story is built around attention diagnostics and end-to-end video quality. INT4 KV-cache quantization remains usable in the reported setting, while INT2 causes severe visual artifacts. The authors show a consistent shift of attention mass toward cached tokens across layers, heads, denoising steps, and prompts, and this shift correlates with poor video quality. Applying the correction brings the attention distribution closer to the unquantized model and improves video-generation benchmarks across multiple models and quantization schemes. The supplied text does not include the full result tables, so exact metric values should not be inferred.","reading_guide":"Skim in 15–25 minutes. on-policy distillation 里的一个细粒度问题：学生模型在自己生成的前缀上接受教师 token 分布监督，但高 teacher-student KL 并不一定意味着这个 token 位置真的能学到东西。作者要区分两类分歧：一种是教师把概率质量重新分配到学生已经认为可能的候选 token 上，更新容易被学生吸收；另一种是教师主要支持学生当前预测支撑集之外的 token，KL budget 分配、以及训练动态诊断都有参考价值，但贡献主要在 post-train/distillation 的信号选择层面，不是新的基座预训练 recipe。","technical_approach":"核心方法有两步。第一步是 fixed-context diagnostic。作者收集学生 on-policy rollout 产生的前缀，把这些前缀固定成 context bank，然后用训练前后的学生模型在同一批上下文上重新打分，计算同一上下文下 teacher-student KL 的下降量。这样可以把 token 位置自身的可学习性从 rollout 噪声、后续上下文漂移和答案级评价中分离出来。第二步是定义 token teachability。作者用学生 top-K token 集合作为局部支撑集，考察教师概率质量是否主要落在这个支撑集或其附近。如果教师纠正信号与学生当前支撑集对齐，那么高 KL 更可能转化为 KL reduction；如果教师质量主要在学生支撑集之外，高 KL 往往只是不可兼容分歧。基于这个诊断，TA-OPD 用 support-aligned teacher-student disagreement 给 token 位置打分，只在高 teachability 的位置施加 OPD loss。它和高熵选择、原始 KL 选择的区别在于：不是选“不确定”或“分歧大”的 token，而是选学生当前状态下更可能被更新吸收的 token。实现上主要依赖 OPD 中已有的教师和学生 token 概率，可以用轻量 top-K 近似，不需要 reward model 或 verifier。","experiments":"实验覆盖 Qwen3 和 Qwen2.5 系列的 teacher-student 配置，包含不同规模、推理能力和 backbone 的组合。诊断实验显示，低熵高 KL 区域并不均质；同样是 confident disagreement，support-aligned 的 token 更能预测固定上下文下的 KL 下降，而 off-support mismatch 的 token 学习收益弱。方法实验中，TA-OPD 在只保留 5% token 的设置下，经常可以匹配或超过 full-token OPD，并优于同 token budget 下的 entropy-based 和 divergence-based 选择方法。这个结果的重点不是单个 benchmark 的提升，而是说明 OPD 的有效性受 token teachability 约束，密集监督并不总是更好。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 25 分钟。先看 Introduction 中 Fig. 1，对 learnable disagreement 和 incompatible disagreement 的例子建立直觉。然后看 Diagnosing Token Teachability，重点是 fixed-context token gain 的定义、为什么要固定上下文、以及 entropy-KL 平面里 Q3 区域被进一步拆开的分析。TA-OPD 的算法部分可以快速看公式和伪代码，关注 teachability score 如何用 top-K 支撑集近似。实验部分优先看 token retention ratio 的消融、和 entropy/KL selector 的对比、不同 K 值的敏感性。附录 C 和 I 如果有时间值得看，分别对应诊断协议和固定上下文收益的理论说明。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks which token-level teacher signals are actually learnable in on-policy distillation. In OPD, the student is supervised on its own rollouts, but a large teacher-student KL at a token does not necessarily imply a useful update. The key distinction is between support-aligned disagreement, where the teacher reallocates probability mass among tokens the student already considers plausible, and off-support disagreement, where the teacher mostly prefers tokens outside the student’s current predictive support.","importance":"The main value is a sharper view of training-signal quality in distillation. It is relevant to LLM post-training, small-model distillation, and the pretrain-posttrain interface because it shows that token loss magnitude is a weak proxy for improvement. The result suggests that token budget should be spent on locally compatible supervision rather than simply dense supervision or high-KL positions. In training experiments, TA-OPD with only 5% retained tokens often matches or surpasses full-token OPD, and improves over entropy-based and divergence-based selectors under the same token budget. Its relevance to foundation-model research is indirect but useful: agentic workloads can be dominated by repeated prefill rather than token generation. If tool-use traces become a larger part of data generation, evaluation, or RL rollouts, stateful inference will materially affect system cost and experiment throughput.","technical_approach":"The central move is to replace stateless requests with stateful sessions. A persistent KV cache is kept across turns, so each request only ingests newly appended tokens, changing the per-turn prefill cost from O(n_t) to O(Δ_t). The design combines several mechanisms: a sequence pool with reusable named sequence IDs; a radix prefix cache that restores cached prefixes through metadata aliasing over a unified KV cache; cell-budget admission and prefix-aware grouped prefill; a prompt-deterministic response cache using prompt-hash seeding; prompt-lookup speculative decoding with concurrency caps; a streaming JSON validator for tool calls; on-device greedy sampling; and RAII-style lifecycle management for session slots. Compared with prefix caching in request-driven systems such as vLLM or SGLang, the key distinction is that session state is treated as a first-class abstraction.","experiments":"The main quantitative workload claim is that consecutive turns in typical 5-turn agent conversations share 85–95% of prompt tokens. The claimed algorithmic gain is delta-only prefill, and shared-prefix grouped prefill can collapse N redundant forward passes into one. from","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T18:12:54+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_26246.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解释并改进 LLM 蒸馏中的一个现象：软标签包含完整 teacher 分布，按直觉应优于硬标签，但实验上硬标签和软标签混合训练常常更好。","importance":"它对 post-train 和小模型压缩更直接，对预训练也有启发。关键点不是“更多分布信息一定更好”，而是训练目标要考虑自回归生成时的分布偏移。纯 soft KD 在 teacher prefix 上拟合得更好，但并不保证 student 在自己生成的 prefix 上稳定。这个观察也适用于预训练数据合成、teacher-generated corpus、SFT 数据蒸馏和小模型 continued pretrain：局部 next-token 拟合与序列级行为之间存在缺口。","technical_approach":"论文提出 Bridge–Garden Decomposition，用局部风险敏感性把生成位置分成两类。Bridge 是高风险位置，下一个 token 需要比较精确；一旦选错，后续轨迹容易偏离。Garden 是低风险位置，多个候选 token 都能保持语义和连贯性。基于这个划分，作者给出 exposure bias 的上界分解，解释 hard 和 soft 的互补性：hard label 会把概率集中到 teacher 实际选择的 token，更适合 Bridge，能减少早期错误引起的级联偏移；soft label 保留 teacher 对多个合理 token 的分布，更适合 Garden，能保持多样性和分布形状。方法上，论文不只用固定比例混合，而提出一组自适应混合策略，包括 confidence-guided、entropy-guided、curriculum-guided 和 risk-guided，把不同 prefix 位置分配给不同程度的 hard/soft 监督。","experiments":"实验覆盖多个 teacher–student 组合：Qwen 7B→3B、Llama 8B→1B、Gemma 4B→1B、DeepSeek-Coder 6.7B→1.3B，并扩展到更大 Qwen2.5 容量差距、Qwen2.5-Coder 和开放式生成。任务包括 commonsense、math 和 coding。核心实验证据有三点：第一，hard+soft 混合稳定优于纯 soft KD；第二，这种提升不是来自更好的 teacher matching，加入 hard 后训练拟合指标反而变差；第三，混合目标降低了 exposure bias，因此序列级生成表现更好。论文还报告其方法优于 divergence-based 和 on-policy baselines，并将训练成本降低 排名更有价值。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a puzzling behavior in LLM distillation: soft labels expose the full teacher distribution and should be more informative, yet mixing soft labels with sampled hard labels often produces better students.","importance":"The immediate relevance is model compression and post-training, but the lesson also matters for pretraining-adjacent workflows. The main point is that better next-token matching under teacher prefixes does not necessarily imply better autoregressive behavior under student-generated prefixes. This distinction applies to teacher-generated corpora, synthetic SFT data, continued pretraining with distilled data, and small-model training recipes that rely on large-model supervision.","technical_approach":"The paper introduces a Bridge–Garden Decomposition based on local risk sensitivity. Bridge positions are high-risk: the next token has to be close to exact, because a wrong choice can push the future trajectory into a bad region. Garden positions are low-risk: several alternatives can preserve meaning and coherence. This decomposition yields an exposure-bias bound with separate contributions from these regions. The explanation is then straightforward: hard-label training concentrates probability on the teacher’s sampled action and is useful in Bridges, while soft-label training preserves the teacher’s distribution over alternatives and is useful in Gardens. The proposed methods therefore adapt the hard/soft mixture by prefix context, using confidence-, entropy-, curriculum-, and risk-guided strategies rather than a single global mixing weight.","experiments":"The experiments cover several teacher–student pairs and model families, including Qwen 7B to 3B, Llama 8B to 1B, Gemma 4B to 1B, and DeepSeek-Coder 6.7B to 1.3B, with additional tests on larger Qwen2.5 capacity gaps, Qwen2.5-Coder, and open-ended generation. Evaluation spans commonsense reasoning, math, and coding. The key empirical findings are: mixed hard/soft supervision consistently outperforms pure soft KD; the gain is not explained by closer teacher matching, since adding hard labels can worsen the training-fit metric; and the mixed objective reduces exposure bias, which improves sequence-level behavior. The paper also reports better results than divergence-based and on-policy baselines, with a 9.7× reduction in training cost, mainly by avoiding more expensive on-policy or heavy distribution-matching procedures.","reading_guide":"Skim-to-careful reading is recommended, roughly 30–45 minutes. Existing training-free KV compression methods usually rank tokens by one importance signal -- attention, recency, layer-wise allocation, or key distinctiveness -- which becomes brittle when useful context is globally distinctive, locally episodic, or immediately relevant. We introduce NestedKV, a key-only KV cache compression method inspired by the Continuum Memory System in Nested Learning. NestedKV ma","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-26T08:14:39+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_26678.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决长上下文推理中的 都只刻画一种“重要性”，在强压缩和超长上下文下容易错删答案相关 token。NestedKV 把 cache eviction 改写为 key 空间中的多时间尺度异常检测，这个角度有助于理解长上下文模型的内部记忆几何，也能为后续长上下文训练目标或注意力结构设计提供信号。","technical_approach":"NestedKV 在 prefill 之后、autoregressive 捕捉近端上下文。每个 token 会根据它相对不同 anchor 的 cosine anomaly 得到多套排名。外层的 training-free learner 再做两件事：一是按 head 调整不同时间尺度排名的权重，让不同 head 偏向不同记忆角色；二是用跨尺度排名分歧作为 surprise signal，在 blended score 和最强单尺度 score 之间平滑路由。最后再配合 Full KV 上限只差 0.2 分，说明短上下文知识能力基本没有被 cache 策略破坏。需要注意的是，结果主要支撑 training-free serving 场景，不能直接推出训练阶段的长程依赖学习能力提升。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 30–45 分钟。先看 Introduction 中对 attention-based 压缩失配的图示和论证，再看 Method 中三类 空间多尺度记忆这一建模视角。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets the memory cost of long-context inference: how to compress the KV cache after prefill while keeping the model, the attention implementation, and the parameters unchanged.","importance":"This is an inference-efficiency paper rather than a pretraining paper, but it is still relevant for long-context foundation model research. Its main point is that a single token-importance signal is brittle. Attention mass, recency, sink preservation, or a single key-distinctiveness score each captures only one notion of memory. Under aggressive compression, answer-bearing tokens may be globally distinctive, locally episodic, or immediately relevant, and these cases are not well served by one anchor. The key-space, multi-timescale view is useful for thinking about memory geometry in long-context models and may inform future training objectives or attention designs.","technical_approach":"NestedKV compresses the cache after prefill and before autoregressive decoding, independently at each Transformer layer. It only decides which cached positions remain; values, model weights, and attention kernels are unchanged. The method builds three key-space anchors: a stable/global anchor for document-level background, an episodic/block-level anchor for local topic shifts, and a current/sliding-window anchor for immediate context. Each token receives cosine-anomaly rankings against these timescales. A training-free outer combiner then adapts the mixture per attention head, upweighting the most discriminative timescale for that head. It also computes cross-scale rank disagreement as a surprise signal and routes each token between the blended score and the strongest individual timescale score. The final selection is paired with adaptive per-head cache budgets, separating token scoring within a head from memory allocation across heads.","experiments":"The main experiments use frozen Qwen3-4B and evaluate on RULER, LongBench, LooGLE, LongBench-E, InfiniteBench, and MMLU-Pro. The reported pattern is strongest under long contexts and aggressive compression. NestedKV is best or close to best across many RULER context-ratio cells. On LongBench, under the paper’s r=0.75 setting, the average score rises from 30.77 to 50.06. On MMLU-Pro, at r=0.25, it stays within 0.2 points of the Full KV result, suggesting that the compression rule does not materially damage short-context knowledge behavior. The evidence mainly supports training-free serving use cases; it should not be read as evidence that the model has learned better long-range dependencies during training.","reading_guide":"Skim in 30–45 minutes. In the experiments, focus on the high-compression long-context RULER cells, the LongBench average, and the MMLU-Pro retention result. We introduce QAM-W (Quadrature Amplitude Modulation for Weights), a codec that recovers this structure: each row is L2-normalized, block-Hadamard rotated, paired into 2D coordinates, and quantized against a single Lloyd-Max codebook trained on the unit circular Gaussian, with activation-aware per-channel scaling. In a cross-model study spanning five LLMs from four families (1.1B--13B parameters) and eight q","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T21:28:46+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_26339.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体但实际的问题：在不重训模型的前提下，如何把 LLM 权重压到约 5–6 bits per weight 的区间，同时尽量不损失语言建模质量。作者指出，主流 PTQ 方法大多按标量独立量化，每个权重单独编码，忽略了同一行权重内部的相关结构；而这部分结构在中等码率区间仍有可利用的率失真冗余。论文想解决的不是极限低比特 4bpw 场景，而是“质量基本守住、存储再往下压一截”的带宽/存储甜点区间。","importance":"对基座研究的直接意义不在预训练方法本身，而在模型部署侧的权重表示设计。它给出一个清楚的结论：在 5–6 bpw 这一档，单纯做更细的标量量化未必是最优，利用轻量正交旋转后做 2D 联合编码，可以在不引入复杂解码结构的情况下吃到一部分相关性红利。论文的贡献量级是中等偏实用：不是改变量化范式的大工作，但把“旋转 + 激活感知缩放 + 小维度向量量化”组合成了一个工程上可落地、分析上也较完整的 codec，并且用跨模型实验把适用区间讲清楚了。另一个有价值的点是作者强调 paired KL 与 perplexity 变化几乎同序，这对量化方法的快速筛选有参考意义。","technical_approach":"方法由四步组成。第一步是行级范数分解：每一行权重先单独抽出 L2 norm，方向向量再单独编码。这样把“半径”和“方向”拆开，便于后续只处理方向失真。第二步是确定性的 block-Hadamard 旋转，并配合 sign mask。这样做不是为了学习一个最优旋转矩阵，而是用一个无需存储、可快速 butterfly 实现的正交变换，把原始坐标打散，使相邻坐标更接近各向同性的高斯对。第三步是把旋转后的相邻两个实数坐标配成一个 2D 点，再按 pair-specific scale 做归一化。这个 scale 不是简单按权重统计量给，而是借鉴 AWQ 的 activation-aware 思路，用层输出 trace identity 推导出的通道重要性来校准；作者用最多 1024 条单位化行做校准，估计每对坐标的尺度。第四步才是编码：核心版本 QAM-W 用一个在单位圆对称高斯上通过 Lloyd 迭代训练出的统一 2D codebook，对每个归一化后的二维点做最近邻查表量化。对照基线则是 polar coding，把幅值和相位分开量化。作者的论点是，若旋转后二维对近似 circular Gaussian，那么直接在二维平面上做 Lloyd-Max 码本，比“幅值 × 相位”分解更贴近真实失真几何，也比独立标量量化保留更多联合结构。论文还给了从 codec distortion 到 KL 的单调复合上界，用来解释为什么局部量化误差会映射到语言模型输出分布的变化。实现上作者刻意把维度固定在 2D，因为 11 bit/pair 的码本大小最多 16KB，可常驻 L1 cache，解码只需一次表查找和缩放，结构上比 additive codebook 或 trellis 类方法简单。","experiments":"实验覆盖五个 LLM、四个家族、参数规模 1.1B 到 13B，并在统一协议下比较八种量化配置。主结果是：activation-aware 的 QAM-W 在约 5.5 bpw 时，WikiText-2 perplexity 相对 BF16 的偏差在所有模型上都控制在 ±0.4% 以内；作者把这个质量区间与 SmoothQuant 的 W8A8 对齐，声称在达到相近质量包线时，权重位宽可少约 32%。这说明它主要赢在权重存储，而不是端到端 W/A 一体量化。论文也明确承认在严格 4 bpw 下，QTIP 更强，因此 QAM-W 的适用点不是极低比特。方法比较上，joint 2D codebook 优于 polar baseline，说明二维联合编码确实比“幅值+相位”分拆更有效；activation-aware scaling 也优于仅靠权重统计的版本，说明误差预算需要向高激活通道倾斜。作者还做了 paired KL 与 ΔPPL 的相关性分析，在 37 个 method-model 组合上得到 Spearman ρ=0.99，几乎可以把 paired KL 当成排序诊断指标。文中还提到统一的六任务 harness，但从给出的信息看，最扎实的证据仍是 perplexity 与 KL，而不是大规模下游任务提升。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三件事。第一，看方法部分里 codec pipeline 的四步：row norm factorization、Hadamard rotation、pair scaling、2D codebook，这四步各自解决什么问题。第二，看作者为什么坚持 2D 而不是更高维向量量化：关键不只是精度，还有码本驻留 L1、解码查表简单、不会把 kernel 复杂度推高。第三，看实验里 5–6 bpw 与 4 bpw 的分界，这决定了方法的真实定位。建议重点看 Introduction、QAM-W Codec Pipeline、与 polar/QTIP/SmoothQuant 的对比结果，以及 paired KL vs ΔPPL 的相关性图表。预计 20–30 分钟能把核心思路和适用边界读明白；如果关心理论，可再看 distortion 到 KL 的分析部分，理解作者如何把局部量化误差和 LM 输出退化联系起来。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a narrow but practical question: how to compress LLM weights into the roughly 5–6 bits-per-weight regime with minimal quality loss, without retraining. The key observation is that most post-training quantizers are scalar and encode each weight independently, which ignores pairwise structure within a weight row. The authors are not trying to win the extreme 4 bpw setting; they focus on the middle regime where quality should remain close to BF16 while storage and bandwidth are pushed down further.","importance":"Its relevance is mainly on the deployment side rather than pretraining itself. The useful takeaway is that in the 5–6 bpw range, scalar quantization is not obviously the right endpoint: a lightweight combination of orthogonal rotation, activation-aware scaling, and 2D joint coding can recover some rate-distortion slack without introducing a complicated decoder. The contribution is moderate but concrete. It is not a new quantization paradigm, but it packages several known ideas into a codec with a clear operating regime and a reasonably complete analysis. Another useful point is the empirical result that paired KL tracks perplexity degradation almost perfectly, which may help with fast screening of quantization schemes.","technical_approach":"$26","experiments":"$27","reading_guide":"For a quick read, focus on three things. audio-video inputs create long token sequences, while naive compression can remove the short acoustic cues and cross-modal alignments needed for answering.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model research is mainly in multimodal token budgeting and the train–inference interface for compressed contexts. The useful lesson is that audio and video should not be compressed independently: decisive evidence often lies in the temporal relation between what is heard and what is seen. The UGC-AVQA benchmark is also useful as an evaluation slice for native multimodal models because it explicitly filters out questions solvable from visual priors alone.","technical_approach":"The work has two components. UGC-AVQA is a user-generated-video benchmark with 1,000 videos and 4,816 QA pairs, targeting audiovisual event progression, scene or temporal transitions, cross-scene alignment, and fine-grained contrast. A key design step is an audio-removal check: after removing the audio track, Gemini-3.1-Flash is asked to answer; only samples that fail under this setting are retained. On the method side, OMAC is a training-free plug-in compression method. It keeps compact visual memory and temporally grounded audio anchors, then allocates audio budget according to the visual memory distribution. This differs from modality-isolated compression because visual structure is used to guide where audio evidence should be preserved. O-MARC adds compression distillation, training the model to reason over memory-compressed multimodal contexts so that inference-time compressed inputs are less out-of-distribution.","experiments":"UGC-AVQA contains a benchmark split with 206 videos and 1,648 QA pairs, while the remaining 794 videos form the training split. The authors manually review and correct 30% of both benchmark candidates and generated training examples. Experiments are reported on Qwen2.5-Omni models at 3B, 7B, and 30B scales. OMAC improves over direct token compression at comparable inference cost. O-MARC gives stronger gains in the 3B setting, and the 7B version outperforms a contemporary training-based compression baseline on the overall average.
