T","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-26T05:50:46+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_26579.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文处理的是 rubric-based RL 中多维奖励聚合不均衡的问题：一些容易满足的维度较早饱和，但静态加权仍持续奖励这些维度，导致模型在总分上升的同时，在关键约束或难优化维度上仍有明显缺陷。","importance":"这篇论文对预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对 post-train 训练信号设计有参考价值。它把开放式生成的奖励问题从“如何构造更好的 judge”转到“如何把 criterion-level feedback 合成成更合理的标量奖励”。这个问题会影响 SFT/RL 阶段的行为塑形，也会反过来影响对预训练能力缺口的诊断。价值主要在 reward scalarization，而不是新的 RL 算法。","technical_approach":"Focal Reward 不改 judge、不改 rubric，也不改 GRPO 一类策略优化框架，只在奖励合成层做干预。方法先用 inverse reward projection 估计每个 rubric criterion 的饱和程度，即判断某个维度在当前 rollout 分布中是否已经接近上限；再根据饱和度调整 criterion 权重。已饱和维度被降权，仍有较大提升空间的维度被加权。这样做的目标是避免静态 scalarizer 把训练信号浪费在 fluency、format compliance 等容易得分的维度上，而忽略 nuanced helpfulness、任务约束满足等更难维度。与固定加权、多维分数简单平均相比，它更像一个在线的奖励方向校准器。","experiments":"实验覆盖 3 个模型规模和 6 个 benchmark，共 18 个 model-benchmark 组合。论文报告 Focal Reward 在全部 18 个比较中超过最强静态聚合基线；相对最强静态 baseline 的平均提升随模型规模约为 +1.38 到 +2.50 分。消融和 rollout 分析支持一个关键结论：不同 rubric 维度在训练中确实以不同速度饱和，在线重分配权重能把优化压力转向仍有 headroom 的维度。需要注意，论文摘要和正文片段显示的结果主要是开放式生成评测分数，尚不足以证明它能稳定改善更硬的可验证任务或长期偏好一致性。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 20–30 分钟。重点看方法部分中 inverse reward projection 如何定义 saturation，以及 adaptive reweighting 如何进入原有 GRPO-style reward。实验部分优先看三类内容：不同 criterion 的饱和轨迹、与 static scalarizer 的逐 benchmark 对比、去掉 saturation-aware 权重后的消融。若只关心预训练，可把它作为 reward shaping 的案例，而不是预训练 recipe。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses reward imbalance in rubric-based RL for open-ended generation. loss 改善会受到上界约束。论文还给出一个关键尺度关系：稳定性约束与 η / ||W|| 相关，其中 η 是学习率，||W|| 是参数范数。这个关系用于解释学习率衰减和 weight decay。学习率 schedule 通过改变有效更新尺度，使训练在 SoSD 后仍能进行更细粒度的下降；weight decay 通过抑制权重范数增长，放松由大范数带来的谱稳定约束，从而允许谱分布继续有更充分的调整。","experiments":"实验覆盖 GPT-2 和 LLaMA 两类 decoder-only 架构。GPT-2 Small 为 124M，GPT-2 Medium 为 355M，在 FineWeb 上使用 modded-nanogpt 训练 recipe；LLaMA 使用 0.5B 和 2B 参数规模，在 C4 上训练。优化器主要是 AdamW，β1=0.9、β2=0.95；GPT-2 Small 还加入 Muon 对比。学习率策略包括 GPT-2 上的 Step Decay 和 Warmup-Stable-Decay，以及 LLaMA 上的 Cosine decay；LLaMA 0.5B 还做了 weight decay 消融。核心实验结果是：在不同模型规模、架构、schedule、weight decay 设置和优化器下，谱分布相似度都比参数相似度更早饱和；SoSD 的开始时间与验证 loss 从快速下降转入慢速下降基本同步。Muon 设置下 SoSD 仍然存在，说明该现象不是 AdamW 特有的副作用，而更像是 Transformer 预训练中的普遍谱动力学特征。weight decay 消融支持作者的机制解释：抑制参数范数增长可以影响 SoSD 约束，从而改变 slow descent 阶段的优化行为。","reading_guide":"建议精读。先看 Introduction 中对问题的定义，确认作者不是在提出新 optimizer，而是在解释预训练动态。然后重点看第 2 节的现象定义和 Figure 1、Figure 2：这里能看到参数相似度与谱分布相似度的分离，以及 SoSD 与 loss plateau 的同步。理论部分建议重点读 Theorem 4.5 和 Theorem 4.9，不必陷入每个证明细节，但要理解假设条件、SoSD 出现条件，以及 loss descent bound 如何依赖 singular distribution variation。预训练工程相关读者应重点看 schedule、weight decay、Muon 的实验与解释，因为这些部分最容易转化为训练监控指标：在真实大规模训练中，定期跟踪归一化奇异值分布可能比只看 loss、grad norm、weight norm 更早暴露训练进入慢速阶段的结构性原因。值得深挖的问题有两个：一是不同矩阵类型是否同步进入 SoSD，例如 attention projection、MLP up/down projection、embedding/head 是否有不同相变时间；二是 SoSD 是否能用于动态调整学习率、weight decay 或 layer-wise training policy。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies the common two-phase trajectory in language-model pretraining: a sharp early loss drop followed by a long slow-descent phase. Its main question is what internal mechanism marks or drives the transition. The proposed answer is Stability of Singular Distribution, or SoSD: the trace-normalized singular-value spectrum of parameter matrices stabilizes early, and the onset of this spectral stability is synchronized with the transition of validation loss into the slow-descent regime.","importance":"The contribution is best viewed as a training-dynamics diagnostic and explanatory framework rather than a new training recipe. It gives pretraining researchers a measurable signal that connects macroscopic loss saturation to the spectral structure of model weights. The useful point is that slow descent does not mean parameters have stopped moving. The entries and directions of weight matrices can continue to change, while the relative singular-value profile has already settled. This distinction offers a cleaner way to reason about learning-rate schedules, weight decay, and optimizers such as Muon.","technical_approach":"$4a","experiments":"The experiments cover decoder-only GPT-2 and LLaMA models. GPT-2 Small has 124M parameters and GPT-2 Medium has 355M parameters, trained on FineWeb with a modded-nanogpt recipe. LLaMA models of 0.5B and 2B parameters are trained on C4. The main optimizer is AdamW with β1=0.9 and β2=0.95; GPT-2 Small also includes Muon runs. The schedule settings include Step Decay and Warmup-Stable-Decay for GPT-2 and Cosine decay for LLaMA, with a weight-decay ablation on LLaMA 0.5B. Across these settings, normalized singular spectra stabilize much earlier than the corresponding weight matrices, and the SoSD onset aligns with the validation-loss transition into slow descent. The persistence of the phenomenon under Muon indicates that SoSD is not merely an AdamW artifact. The weight-decay ablation supports the proposed mechanism: controlling weight-norm growth changes the spectral stability constraint and thereby affects late-stage descent.","reading_guide":"Read carefully. Start with the introduction to understand that the paper is about pretraining dynamics, not about proposing a new optimizer. Then focus on the section defining SoSD and on Figures 1 and 2, where the separation between weight alignment and spectral-distribution alignment is most visible. In the theory section, prioritize Theorem 4.5 and Theorem 4.9: the important parts are the assumptions, the emergence condition for SoSD, and the way loss-descent bounds depend on singular-distribution variation. For pretraining practice, the most relevant sections are the analyses of learning-rate schedules, weight decay, and Muon. A natural follow-up is to inspect whether different matrix classes enter SoSD at different times, such as attention projections versus MLP projections, and whether SoSD can be used as a trigger for adaptive scheduling or layer-wise optimization policies.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"应看谱指标定义和 optimizer 对照；若复现成立，预训练慢相位不该只按 LR schedule 解释。","en":"Read the spectral metric and optimizer controls; if it reproduces, the slow phase of pretraining should not be explained only through LR schedules."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Hongtao Zhang","Wenjie Zhou","Chenxi Jia","Wei Chen","Xueqi Cheng"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["pretraining-dynamics","singular-values","optimization","Muon","WSD"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26489","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.26489","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.26379","title":"When Does LeJEPA Learn a World Model?","abstract":"A representation that scrambles the true degrees of freedom of the world cannot support reliable planning or compositional generalization. We prove that LeJEPA (alignment plus Gaussian regularization) linearly recovers the world's latent variables from nonlinear observations, a property known as linear identifiability, in a broad class of worlds where latents evolve under stationary, additive-noise transitions. Our main result is that among all such worlds, the Gaussian is the unique latent JEPA/联合嵌入预测从经验方法推进到可分析对象，补上了“防塌缩后到底学到了什么”这一空白。第二，它给出一个很具体的结论：LeJEPA 的 alignment + Gaussian regularization 组合，不只是稳定训练技巧，而是在高斯潜变量、平稳加性噪声转移下，确实偏好线性可恢复的潜空间；这让“高斯化表示分布”从工程启发变成了有理论支撑的设计。第三，它的 if-and-only-if 结论很尖锐：在作者定义的这类世界中，高斯是唯一能保证线性可识别的潜变量分布。这一点对表征学习、世界模型、latent planning 都有启发，因为它说明 identifiability 不是 JEPA 普遍自带的性质，而是目标函数与数据生成假设共同决定的。对做预训练的人来说，价值不在于可直接照搬到 LLM，而在于它提供了一个分析模板：显式分布正则 + 预测一致性，可能比隐式防塌缩更容易得到可解释的训练偏置。","technical_approach":"方法上，论文分析的是 LeJEPA 目标：一部分是 alignment，让正样本对的表示接近；另一部分是把表示分布显式正则到各向同性高斯，文中对应 SIGReg 一类 Gaussian regularization。作者先定义一类世界：潜变量各维独立，正样本来自平稳过程，转移是加性噪声。然后在前向定理里进一步专门化到 z~N(0,I)，并利用平稳性推出唯一保持边缘分布不变的加性噪声转移是 OU 形式 z'=ρz+sqrt(1-ρ^2)ε。关键技术点是把复合映射 h=f∘g 在高斯测度下做谱分解。直观上，若 h 含有高于一次的非线性成分，这些成分在 alignment 目标下会受到严格惩罚，而 Gaussian regularization 又要求输出分布保持标准高斯，因此最优解只能保留一次项，也就是线性映射 Qz。这里的证明思路和高斯空间上的正交多项式/谱展开关系很深：不同“非线性阶数”在相关高斯对上有不同衰减系数，alignment 会把高阶项压下去。反方向定理更有意思：作者证明在同一类平稳加性噪声世界里，若潜变量分布不是高斯，就无法对所有此类世界都保证 LeJEPA 学到线性可识别表示。也就是说，高斯不是一个方便的例子，而是唯一能给出这类普适保证的分布。除此之外，论文还给出 approximate identifiability 结果：当 alignment 和 Gaussian matching 只近似满足时，表示到线性映射的偏差可以被上界控制；并进一步证明，若表示线性恢复潜变量，则在该表示空间做规划足以达到潜空间最优规划。这把“可识别”与“可用于控制”连了起来，而不是停留在表征美学层面。","experiments":"实验部分主要是理论结论的验证，而不是追求大规模任务成绩。文中做了几类实验。第一类是合成混合实验：在 2D 非线性 mixing 上，检查 LeJEPA 学到的表示是否与真实潜变量线性对应，并与非高斯潜变量分布做对照，验证高斯条件的重要性。第二类是高维实验，潜变量维度扩展到 1024 维，用来说明结论不是低维玩具现象。第三类是分布消融，系统替换潜变量分布，观察当偏离高斯时线性可识别性如何恶化，对应理论里的 converse 和 approximate bound。第四类是像素级机器人控制/latent-space planning 实验，用来支撑“线性可识别足以支持最优潜空间规划”的命题。摘要里没有给出完整数值表，提供的全文片段也未包含所有结果表格，因此不宜编造具体提升百分比；但从实验设计可以看出，作者重点不是比基线多几个点，而是验证三件事：高斯条件的必要性、近似满足目标时的平滑退化、以及表征可识别性与规划性能之间的联系。阅读时应重点看图中表示与潜变量的线性对应关系、不同分布下 probe/规划指标的变化，以及近似界是否与经验误差趋势一致。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合按“定义—定理—实验验证”来读。先看 Introduction 和问题设定，明确作者所说的 world model 不是泛指预测未来，而是能线性恢复潜变量。然后看 The World and the Learner，特别是三条世界假设：独立潜变量、平稳性、加性噪声，以及为什么高斯潜变量会导出 OU 转移。真正的核心在理论部分：前向定理里，注意作者如何利用高斯空间谱分解说明高阶非线性会被 alignment 惩罚；反向定理里，注意“高斯唯一性”是如何建立的。若时间有限，优先看主定理、证明思路图、近似可识别性命题和 latent planning 命题，再回头看实验。实验部分建议重点看 2D 分钟，若要吃透证明需要更久。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a stricter question than whether LeJEPA learns useful features: when does it learn a representation that qualifies as a world model? The criterion is linear identifiability. If observations are generated by an unknown nonlinear map x=g(z), the learned representation y=f(x) should recover the latent state up to a linear transform, i.e. h(z)=f(g(z))=Qz. This rules out representations that preserve task performance while arbitrarily entangling the true degrees of freedom. The central problem is to establish the first identifiability result for a JEPA-style objective, characterize exactly when it holds, when it fails, and how it degrades under approximate optimization.","importance":"$4b","technical_approach":"$4c","experiments":"$4d","reading_guide":"$4e","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看定理假设和 converse：它不是证明 JEPA 普遍学世界模型，而是把可识别性压到 Gaussian latent 这个窄条件里。","en":"Read the theorem assumptions and the converse: this does not prove JEPA generally learns world models; it narrows identifiability to Gaussian latents."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Meta AI","New York University"],"authors":["David Klindt","Yann LeCun","Randall Balestriero"],"notable_authors":["Yann LeCun"],"tags":["JEPA","world-models","identifiability","self-supervised-learning","representation-learning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26379","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.26379","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.26795","title":"What Makes Chain-of-Thought Work at Probe Time? Local Co-occurrence Rather Than Global Derivation","abstract":"Chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting reliably improves language-model accuracy, but which properties of a rationale text drive the improvement is poorly understood. Prior work has largely studied generation-time behavior. We instead ask a probe-time question: given a fixed rationale in context, what in that text changes the answer? We identify two complementary sources of the gain. First, even a globally word-shuffled rationale substantially outperforms the no-rationale baseline, indicating a strong","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-26T10:10:01+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_26795.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的不是“模型如何生成 CoT”，而是“当一段 rationale 已经放进上下文后，模型究竟利用了其中什么信息来改进答案预测”。作者要拆开的核心问题是：CoT 的 probe-time 增益，到底主要来自可读的全局推导结构，还是来自更局部的统计线索。全文给出的结论很明确：收益可以分成两部分，一部分是词汇层面的 lexical activation，只要把与题目和答案相关的词放进去，哪怕全局打乱，性能也会明显高于无 rationale；另一部分来自极短程的 local co-occurrence，保留 2–3 token 的连续窗口，往往就能恢复大部分从“词袋”到“完整 CoT”的额外收益。换句话说，probe model 在读取 rationale 时，并不总是依赖句级甚至步级的逻辑顺序。","importance":"对预训练和推理研究的启发主要有三点。第一，它提醒人们不要把 CoT 增益直接等同于“显式逐步推理被模型执行了”。至少在 probe-time 这一侧，局部模式匹配和短程共现可能占了比预想更大的比重。第二，这个结果会影响对 reasoning data、scratchpad、rationale distillation 的理解：如果很多收益来自局部统计结构，那么训练时学到的可能不只是可执行推导，也包括一套高密度的提示模板和局部触发器。第三，它对机制分析和评测设计有直接意义。若 benchmark 只看加不加 CoT 的最终准确率，很容易把“利用 rationale 中的局部线索”误判成“完成了全局推理”。这篇工作不是预训练 recipe 论文，但它对理解语言模型在上下文中如何消费 reasoning text 很有价值，尤其适合作为 CoT 机理研究的反例材料。","technical_approach":"方法设计相当干净。作者采用 generator–probe 框架：先由一个 generator 生成完整 CoT，再把这段 rationale 作为 assistant response 注入 probe model 的上下文，让 probe 只负责读入这段文本并输出最终答案。这样可以把“生成 rationale 的能力”和“利用 rationale 的能力”分开。为了避免最直接的答案泄漏，作者会自动删除显式 answer declaration 句子，再做扰动。\n\n核心实验是一组逐级破坏结构的 perturbation。Sentence Shuffle 保留句内内容但打乱句序，用来测试句级推导顺序是否关键；Word Shuffle 把所有词全局打乱，只保留 token inventory，用来测 lexical activation；更关键的是 n-gram shuffle：把 rationale 切成长度为 n 的连续 token 窗口，窗口内部顺序保留、窗口之间随机打乱。n=1 相当于词袋，n 增大则逐步恢复局部结构。作者观察恢复曲线，寻找最小的 n*，即恢复大部分额外收益所需的最短局部窗口。\n\n为了排除替代解释，全文还做了几类控制。其一，排除 explicit answer copying：删除答案声明句，并检查收益是否仍在。其二，排除 option anchors：不是简单因为 rationale 中重复了选项词。其三，排除 generic reference text：不是任意相关文本都能带来同样收益。其四，测试 question-text perturbation，说明“2–3 token 足够”的现象更像是 CoT rationale 的特殊性质，而不是模型对任意输入都只看极短局部窗口。作者还在多个 generator/probe 组合、不同模型家族、不同参数规模和多个数据集上验证了模式稳定性。","experiments":"文中最有代表性的例子来自 Config D：Qwen3.5-35B-A3B 生成 rationale，Qwen2.5-14B 作为 probe。这个配置里 CoT 相对 input-output baseline 的增益最明显，因此更容易看清扰动效应。引言给出的示例数字很说明问题：IO 为 50.9%，完整 CoT 为 83.9%；Sentence Shuffle 仍有 83.1%，几乎不掉，说明句级顺序贡献很小；Word Shuffle 还有 66.5%，说明仅词汇集合就带来很大提升；当只保留 2-token 连续窗口时，准确率到 76.5%，已经恢复了从词袋到完整 CoT 之间的大部分差距。\n\n实验设置上，每个条件汇总 3 个随机种子、每个 seed 500 个样本，总计 n=1500，并用双侧 McNemar 检验做显著性分析。全文强调，这种 n-gram 恢复曲线在多个模型家族、开闭源配置和不同 benchmark 上都保持定性一致，最小恢复窗口通常在 2–3 token 左右。控制实验显示，这个现象不能主要由答案复制、选项锚点或一般性的词汇重叠解释。另一个重要观察是，对问题文本做类似扰动时，并不会出现同样强的“小窗口即可恢复”现象，说明 CoT 文本确实携带一种特殊的局部可利用结构。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但建议把方法和主实验看仔细。优先看 Introduction 里的 Table 1 和作者对三种 shuffle 条件的解释，这里已经把主要发现讲清楚。然后看 Experimental Setup，重点理解 generator–probe 设计为何能隔离 probe-time 效应，以及作者如何去掉显式答案泄漏。接着看 n-gram perturbation 的主结果图，关注恢复曲线而不是单点数值：关键不是 n=2 恰好多少分，而是收益如何从 word bag 快速逼近 full CoT。最后看控制实验部分，尤其是 answer copying、option anchors、generic text 和 question perturbation，这些决定了结论是否站得住。\n\n如果阅读时间有限，30–40 分钟足够抓住主线。若想深挖，值得继续想两个问题：一是这种局部共现效应在更强 reasoning model 上是否会减弱；二是它与预训练阶段学到的短程统计偏好、attention 局部性、以及 instruction tuning 中 rationale 模板化之间是什么关系。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a neglected side of chain-of-thought: not how a model generates a rationale, but how a model uses a rationale once that text is already placed in context. The central question is whether probe-time CoT gains come from reconstructing a coherent global derivation, or from much more local textual cues. The main finding is that the gain decomposes into two parts. One is lexical activation: even a globally shuffled rationale, which preserves only the token inventory, still improves accuracy substantially over the no-rationale baseline. The other is short-range local co-occurrence: preserving contiguous windows of only 2–3 tokens recovers most of the remaining gap between a word bag and the full rationale. In many settings, sentence-level ordering contributes surprisingly little.","importance":"This matters because it changes how CoT improvements should be interpreted. A gain from adding rationale text does not automatically mean the model is executing a faithful step-by-step derivation at inference time. Part of the benefit may come from local statistical structure that acts as a strong cue for answer prediction. That has implications for reasoning-data design, rationale distillation, and mechanistic analysis. If much of the benefit is carried by local patterns, then some of what models learn from reasoning traces may be reusable prompt structure rather than executable reasoning alone. It also matters for evaluation: benchmark gains from CoT prompting can overstate genuine compositional reasoning if the model is exploiting local rationale cues. The paper is not a pretraining recipe paper, but it is useful for anyone trying to understand how language models consume reasoning text in context.","technical_approach":"$4f","experiments":"$50","reading_guide":"$51","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"反常识的CoT机制结论，实验设计干净，重点看§3的控制变量实验和核心结果表即可","en":"Counterintuitive CoT mechanism findings with clean controlled experiment design, focus on §3 and core result tables only"},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Xiang Wang","Wei Wei"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["chain_of_thought","mechanism_analysis","prompting","lexical_activation"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26795","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.26795","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.26789","title":"Composition Collapse: Stable Factual Knowledge Does Not Imply Compositional Reasoning","abstract":"Post-training is routinely evaluated through aggregate benchmark scores that treat multi-hop reasoning as a single capability -- as if a model that answers more questions correctly must be better at assembling facts. We show that this assumption can be misleading: recipes with statistically indistinguishable atomic knowledge produce composition behaviour separated by over 40 percentage points, a phenomenon we call composition collapse: the systematic failure to assemble stably-known facts into c","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-26T10:01:01+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_26789.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的是一个评测层面的混淆：多跳题答错，到底是因为模型没有稳定掌握原子事实，还是因为它明明知道这些事实，却无法把它们组合成链式推理。现有做法通常直接看 multi-hop accuracy，或者看从 single-hop 到 multi-hop 的 compositionality gap，但这会把“知识提取不稳定”和“组合失败”混在一起。作者提出的 composition collapse，指的是在 atomic knowledge 几乎匹配的情况下，不同 post-training recipe 仍可能在组合能力上拉开很大差距，而且这种差距会被常规 aggregate metric 掩盖。","importance":"对预训练研究者而言，这篇文章最有价值的地方不在于提出了新的训练方法，而在于把一个常被误读的能力拆开测量。它说明 post-training 的收益至少可以来自三条不同通道：让原子知识更稳定、让已知事实更容易被组合、以及把可组合的深度往更长链条推进。这个分解对理解 SFT、RLHF、outcome-verified RL、reasoning trace distillation 的差异很有帮助。更重要的是，作者做了 same-base 的受控 LoRA 干预，尽量把 base model 差异拿掉，结果显示训练目标本身就会显著影响 residual composition failure。对做 pretrain/post-train 接口的人，这意味着不能只看最终 benchmark 分数；如果评测没有控制 atomic access，就很难判断一个 recipe 到底是在补知识、补检索稳定性，还是在补真正的组合过程。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 double-gate protocol。对一个深度为 k 的组合问题，作者先为每个原子事实构造多种 paraphrased probes，再构造与主链条对齐的 sub-question。一个样本只有在两个门都通过后，才进入 residual composition failure 的统计：\n\n第一道 atomic gate 检查每个原子事实是否能在多种改写下稳定回答正确。这里不是“答对一次就算知道”，而是要求跨 paraphrase 一致，目的是过滤掉不稳定的知识提取。第二道 sub-question gate 检查每个子问题在单独提问时是否都能答对，确保模型不仅“存着事实”，而且能在局部链条位置把它 verbalize 出来。若通过两道门后主问题仍然答错，这部分才被记为 residual composition failure。\n\n在这个定义上，作者把 post-training 收益分成三条通道：Δatom 表示 atomic stability 的变化，Δcomp 表示在原子稳定性匹配条件下 residual composition failure 的变化，Δdepth 表示 residual failure 首次超过 50% 的深度阈值变化。这个分解很实用，因为它把“知道更多事实”和“更会把事实串起来”分开了。\n\n数据方面，作者构建了 D4v2，一个 390 个主问题、2490 个 atomic sub-question 的 temporal benchmark，覆盖四类任务、深度 2–11。实验对象包括 7–13B 的六个开源模型，涵盖 base、RLHF、SFT reasoning-trace distillation、native outcome-verified RL 等 recipe。更关键的是，作者还做了同一 base model 上的 LoRA 受控实验，对比 SFT-answer、SFT-trace 和 GRPO，尽量隔离训练目标而不是模型底座差异。全文还讨论了一个重要边界：residual composition failure 仍然是“上界”，因为格式问题、干扰项注意力、greedy decoding 轨迹退化等也可能让主问题失败，所以作者在后文专门量化这部分 margin。","experiments":"最关键的结果有四个。第一，base model 在这个协议下几乎过不了 atomic gate。作者指出，在任意深度上，base 模型被 atomic knowledge 过滤掉，atomic gate 通过的样本数通常少于 5 个。这说明很多所谓的 multi-hop 失败，首先是稳定知识访问不足，而不是组合本身。\n\n第二，在 atomic stability 匹配到 2 个百分点以内时，不同 post-training recipe 的 residual composition failure 仍可在 depth 2 拉开 0% 到 47% 的差距。也就是说，原子知识几乎一样，组合表现却完全不同，这正是 composition collapse。作者特别强调，outcome-verified RL 在相同数据和计算预算下，明显优于 SFT trace distillation。\n\n第三，训练某一特定 composition depth，通常能显著降低该深度上的 residual failure，但对相邻未见深度的迁移很弱。这说明模型学到的不是一个平滑外推的“通用组合算法”，而更像是对特定链长或结构的适配。\n\n第四，允许模型使用 chain-of-thought 后，residual failure 会下降 3–5 倍。这个结果很重要，因为它把瓶颈部分定位到 generation-time computation，而不只是静态知识表征。换言之，模型可能已经具备所需原子事实，但若不给足够的推理展开空间，组合过程仍会失败。\n\n从实验设计看，作者同时比较了不同家族模型，也做了 same-base LoRA 干预，因此结论不只是“某个模型更强”，而是“某类训练目标在控制知识稳定性后，对组合通道有不同影响”。","reading_guide":"这篇虽然标成 skim，但如果你关心 reasoning eval 或 post-train recipe，值得比普通 skim 多花一点时间。先看 Introduction，重点抓住作者为何反对直接用 compositionality gap 代表组合能力。然后看 Method 和 Figure 1，把 double-gate protocol、residual composition failure、三通道分解这几个定义吃透；这是全文最重要的部分。接着看主结果表，优先关注“atomic stability 匹配后”的 recipe 对比，而不是原始总分。再看 same-base LoRA intervention，这部分最能说明训练目标本身的作用。最后看 CoT 开关实验和 depth transfer 结果，它们分别对应“生成时计算是否重要”和“组合能力是否可外推”两个关键问题。\n\n如果时间有限，45 分钟左右可以读完主线。若继续深挖，建议思考两点：一是 double-gate 是否还能扩展到非时间类、多域组合任务；二是 residual composition failure 中有多少仍是 decoding 或格式问题，而不是内部表示层面的组合缺陷。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a measurement problem in multi-hop reasoning evaluation. When a model fails a compositional question, the failure can come from two very different sources: unstable access to the underlying atomic facts, or a genuine inability to compose facts that are already stably available. Standard aggregate metrics, including raw multi-hop accuracy and the usual compositionality gap, conflate these two failure modes. The paper calls the hidden second phenomenon composition collapse: large differences in compositional behavior that remain after atomic knowledge is controlled for, and that are largely invisible under aggregate evaluation.","importance":"Its main value is diagnostic rather than algorithmic. The paper gives a cleaner way to separate what post-training is improving: factual stability, composition conditioned on stable facts, or the depth at which composition breaks down. That matters for interpreting SFT, RLHF, reasoning-trace distillation, and outcome-verified RL. For researchers working on the pretrain–post-train interface, the message is simple: benchmark gains on multi-hop tasks are not enough to tell whether a recipe improved knowledge access or actual composition. The controlled same-base LoRA intervention is especially useful, because it suggests that the training objective itself can materially change residual composition failure even when the base model is held fixed.","technical_approach":"$52","experiments":"$53","reading_guide":"$54","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"后训练评估的重要反常识发现，双门分解方法可直接复用在内部对齐效果评估中，重点看§3的协议设计","en":"Important counterintuitive finding on post-training evaluation, the double-gate decomposition protocol can be directly reused for internal alignment effect evaluation, focus on §3 protocol design"},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Zhe Yu","Wenpeng Xing","Yunzhao Wei","Jie Chen","Hongzhi Wang","Xuyang Teng","Meng Han"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["compositional_reasoning","post_training_analysis","evaluation_protocol","factual_knowledge"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26789","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.26789","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.26772","title":"Beyond a Single Direction: Chain-of-Thought Disrupts Simple Steering of Refusal","abstract":"Large reasoning models (LRMs) generate chain-of-thought (CoT) traces before producing final outputs, introducing a dynamic internal state that may complicate control mechanisms such as refusal. no longer be controlled by a single residual-stream direction (as shown for instruction-tuned LLMs). The paper asks whether CoT generation actively mediates and reconstructs the refusal state, thereby weakening standard activation-steering interventions.","importance":"This is primarily mechanistic/safety work rather than pretraining. The actionable takeaway for post-training of R1/o1-style models is that alignment/refusal can become a dual-channel mechanism: (1) a residual-stream “refusal direction” and (2) the generated CoT itself. If CoT can rebuild refusal during generation, then output-only control (or single-point steering) may be brittle; the reasoning trace becomes both a control target and an attack surface. That has implications for RL-style alignment objectives and for whether to supervise/regularize intermediate traces.","technical_approach":"The contribution is an intervention design that isolates the causal role of CoT.\n- Direction extraction: difference-in-means (Arditi et al.) on residual-stream activations of DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B, using 100 refused harmful vs 100 complied harmless prompts. Activations are taken at template token endpoints (EOI: end-of-instruction; EOT: end-of-think) across layers to define a refusal/compliance direction.\n- Steering: activation addition along the extracted direction with magnitude α.\n- CoT conditions: (i) keep CoT fixed, (ii) suppress/remove CoT, (iii) regenerate CoT under steering in a two-stage setup (generate CoT, then final answer).\nThe method is standard steering; the novelty is treating CoT as an active mediator and testing it with controlled generation protocols.","experiments":"On DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B with 100 harmful JAILBREAKBENCH prompts (0% baseline compliance under greedy decoding):\n- Steering with fixed CoT reverses refusal only ~39% (EOI) to ~43% (EOT), varying by layer.\n- Removing CoT increases compliance to ~70%, suggesting CoT generation counteracts steering by reconstructing refusal.\n- Regenerating CoT under steering yields ~94% compliance.\n- Reusing a previously steered “compliant CoT” without steering still preserves ~48% of the effect, indicating CoT content carries an independent, transferable compliance signal.\nOverall, results support a redundant/parallel mechanism: residual direction + CoT dynamics jointly determine refusal.","reading_guide":"Skim plan (20–30 min):\n1) The exact definitions of the three CoT conditions (fixed vs suppressed vs regenerated); We introduce Contras","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T14:29:36+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_25902.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在只具备“灰盒”访问（只能拿到输出 logits/概率分布、无权重/无训练数据/无内部激活）的条件下，如何审计一个窄域 finetune 过的 LLM：不仅判断它被灌输了什么领域，还要能把被植入的具体事实（实体、数字、模板句式等）逐字恢复出来。","importance":"对预训练/后训练闭环的直接启发在于：finetune 会在模型的无条件先验里留下可被系统性放大的“可读痕迹”，而且不需要白盒激活差分也能被提取。这把“模型审计/数据溯源/合规验证”从内部可解释性问题，部分转化成了纯输出层面的统计对比问题。更关键的是它展示了合成数据管线的指纹可以端到端泄露（生成器模式坍塌→语料伪影→权重印记→输出提取），这会反过来影响预训练数据治理：合成数据的多样性、模板污染、persona 复用等问题不只是质量问题，也可能变成可被外部探测的安全与隐私风险。","technical_approach":"CDD（Contrastive Decoding Diffing）把“提取 finetune prior”拆成三个互补部件：\n1) Simulator：绕开 chat template，用原始 next-token prediction 方式跑模型。动机是 chat 模板与指令对齐会把分布强行拉回“有用、相关、礼貌”的对话域，抑制无条件先验里那点 finetune 偏置；而审计要看的恰恰是先验偏置。\n2) The Void：用“最大程度含糊”的 prefill 作为种子，让 base model 处于高熵、低承诺状态（不把话题钉死到任何领域）。直觉是：当 base 的条件分布很分散时，finetune 引入的微小 logit 偏移更容易在采样过程中主导后续轨迹。\n3) Contrastive Decoding：逐 token 对比 finetuned 与 base 的 logit 分布差异，并把差异放大到解码决策里，同时保留一定的“可置信/可读”约束，避免纯差分导致乱码。它相当于把“两个模型的 KL/对数似然比信号”在线注入到生成过程，使得输出沿着最能区分 finetune 与 base 的方向展开。\n与 ADL 的区别：ADL 依赖白盒激活差分、层选择、探针语料与超参搜索，且更像在隐空间做 steering；CDD 完全在输出层做 diffing，不需要任何 per-model tuning，并且目标从“域描述”提升到“逐字事实恢复”。","experiments":"主要在 SDF（Synthetic Document Finetuning）基准上评测：覆盖 5 个语义差异较大的 finetune 目标、4 个模型家族、规模 1B–32B。论文声称在单一默认配置、零调参条件下，CDD 在不同模型/规模/领域上实现“近乎普遍”的 verbatim recovery，并在表 2 中整体优于需要更强访问权限的 ADL。\n额外验证两点：\n- 生成器指纹：SDF 语料由指令模型生成，存在 mode collapse，把同一个虚构 persona（Dr. Elena Rodriguez）复用到多个无关领域。CDD 能从极其含糊的 prefill 中无条件抽出该 persona，说明泄露的不只是目标事实，还有数据管线伪影。\n- 真实域 finetune 泛化：在 CLadder、COPA、Corr2Cause、TRAM 等因果推理数据集的单数据集非 CoT finetune 上，CDD 近乎完美恢复；混合数据集设置下能正确识别 4 个数据集，并恢复训练提示模板与合成实体等“训练数据痕迹”。（文中给出 exact/near-perfect 的定性结论，但你如果要复现或做安全评估，需要回到实验章节逐项核对 SDF rubric 与具体成功率口径。）","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 30–45 分钟）：\n- 先看方法章节对 Simulator/Void/Contrastive Decoding 的动机解释：它们分别在对抗“chat 对齐抑制”“条件熵不足”“差分信号太弱/不可读”三个问题。\n- 重点看主结果表（文中提到 Table 2）和 SDF 的 SDF rubric（verbatim 的判定标准），确认“恢复”到底是 exact string match、还是允许格式漂移。\n- 再看生成器指纹那一节（文中提到 §6）：这是对预训练数据治理最有外溢价值的部分，建议关注 persona 泄露是如何跨领域出现的，以及是否与解码策略/温度有关。\n- 最后快速扫真实域 finetune（§7）：看它恢复了哪些 prompt 模板/实体，判断对现实审计（比如供应链合规、数据来源争议）是否可迁移。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"How to audit narrowly fine-tuned LLMs and recover the exact implanted content (entities, numbers, templates) when you only have grey-box access to output logits—no weights, no training data, no internal activations.","importance":"The paper reframes a chunk of model auditing from a white-box interpretability problem into an output-level distribution comparison problem. Practically, it suggests that fine-tuning leaves extractable traces in the model’s unconditional prior, and those traces can be amplified without any internal access. The more consequential takeaway for pretraining/data governance is the demonstrated end-to-end “pipeline fingerprinting” chain: synthetic-data generator artifacts (e.g., persona reuse due to mode collapse) can be imprinted into weights via fine-tuning and later extracted externally. That turns synthetic-data diversity and template hygiene into not just quality concerns but also provenance/security risks.","technical_approach":"Contrastive Decoding Diffing (CDD) combines three components:\n1) Simulator: run the model in raw next-token prediction mode, bypassing chat templates. Rationale: instruction/chat formatting anchors generation to a helpful conversational manifold, suppressing the unconditional prior where fine-tuning bias lives.\n2) The Void: start from maximally vague prefills to keep the base model in a high-entropy state. When the conditional distribution is diffuse, small fine-tuning-induced logit shifts can dominate the sampling trajectory.\n3) Contrastive decoding: at each step, compute and amplify the logit-space difference between the fine-tuned model and its base counterpart, while maintaining plausibility constraints to avoid degenerate “difference-only” gibberish.\nCompared to Activation Difference Lens (ADL), CDD removes the need for white-box activations, layer selection, probe corpora, and per-model hyperparameter search, and it targets verbatim fact recovery rather than coarse domain descriptions.","experiments":"Evaluated primarily on the SDF benchmark (Synthetic Document Finetuning): 5 diverse fine-tuning targets, 4 model families, 1B–32B parameters. With a single default configuration (no tuning), CDD reports near-universal verbatim recovery and consistently outperforms ADL (referenced as Table 2) despite requiring strictly less access.\nTwo notable extensions:\n- Generator fingerprinting: because SDF data is LLM-generated and exhibits mode collapse (reused fictional persona “Dr. Elena Rodriguez” across unrelated domains), CDD can extract this persona unconditionally from vague prompts—evidence that unintended pipeline artifacts are recoverable.\n- Real-domain fine-tuning: on causal reasoning datasets (CLadder, COPA, Corr2Cause, TRAM), CDD achieves near-perfect recovery for single-dataset non-CoT fine-tunes; in mixed-dataset settings it correctly identifies all datasets and recovers verbatim prompt templates and synthetic entities from training traces.","reading_guide":"Skim plan (30–45 minutes):\n- Read the method section for the motivation behind Simulator/Void/contrastive decoding; each addresses a distinct failure mode (chat prior suppression, insufficient entropy, weak/unreadable diff signal).\n- Inspect the main results table and the SDF “verbatim” rubric to understand what counts as recovery (exact match vs tolerant criteria).\n- Prioritize the generator fingerprinting section: it has the clearest implications for synthetic data pipelines and provenance.\n- Then skim the real-domain fine-tuning section to judge transferability to practical auditing (template/entity leakage patterns).","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇不改 pretrain recipe，但会改变你对 finetune memorization 可审计性的预期；先看方法三招和对 ADL 的对比，若结果稳，安全评估流程得补这一项。","en":"It does not change pretraining, but it should update your prior on how auditable finetune memorization is; read the three-part method and the ADL comparison first."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Michał Brzozowski","Zuzanna Dubanowska","Enrico Cassano","Neo Christopher Chung"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["memorization","model-auditing","contrastive-decoding","finetuning","black-box-analysis"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25902","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.25902","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.27033","title":"Tracing Computation Density in LLMs","abstract":"Transformer-based large language models (LLMs) are comprised of billions of parameters arranged in deep and wide computational graphs, but it is not clear that they exploit their full capacity for all inputs. We introduce the s-Trace method to efficiently estimate the subgraph of size s that best approximates a full model output. With this method, we find the computation in a variety of LLMs to be organized in two distinct phases. It turns input-dependent compute allocation from an engineering intuition into something that can be estimated and compared. The finding that 7B–14B models show similar compute-density patterns on similar inputs, and that density correlates with output entropy and word rarity, is relevant to dynamic routing, early exiting, sparse activation, MoE routing, and difficulty-aware compute allocation during training. The paper does not propose a new pretraining recipe, but it gives a useful lens for studying how trained models spend computation.","technical_approach":"The proposed method is s-Trace. For a given input and the full model’s output distribution, it estimates a subgraph of size s that best reconstructs that distribution. The method builds on automated circuit discovery and the Information Flow Route framework, but repurposes them from task-specific circuit identification to generic output reconstruction. By sweeping the subgraph budget s, the authors obtain a compute-performance curve: how much of the full prediction distribution can be recovered as more nodes are added. The important choice is to optimize reconstruction of the full probability distribution, rather than only checking whether the top predicted token is preserved.","experiments":"The study analyzes 10 LLMs in the 7B to 14B range. The main empirical result is a two-phase organization of computation. In the construction phase, starting around 10^-4 of the full model size and becoming stable near 10^-3, a small core dominated by early-layer nodes has large marginal impact and can recover the head of the output distribution. In the refinement phase, beyond roughly 10^-2 of the full graph, the trace has already reconstructed about 40% to 60% of the prediction probability mass. Additional nodes, increasingly from later layers and attention heads, provide smaller refinements to the full distribution. The paper also reports that compute density is positively correlated with output entropy and increases for rarer words.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 25 to 35 minutes. It separates a structural limitation of text-only modeling from finite data, model capacity, and optimization. The analysis gives a clean way to talk about why retrieval, tools, programmatic checks, or human review can change the relevant conditional distribution instead of merely appending more tokens. The limitation is that the model is deliberately toy-sized and does not translate into a concrete training objective, data recipe, or scaling result.","technical_approach":"The construction uses a binary mixed-regime process. Tokens are in {0, 1}. In one hidden regime, the sequence alternates deterministically; in the other, the next token is an independent fair coin. The paper distinguishes the full conditional distribution given text and latent state, the text-only marginal obtained by integrating out the latent state, and the model-induced distribution. It then assumes an ideal predictor that exactly matches the text-only marginal, so any remaining error is not an estimation error. The central quantity is a sufficiency gap: the entropy mismatch between the true latent-state conditional and the text-only marginal prediction. An external observer is modeled as a grounding signal with fidelity gamma. The posterior update yields a dominance threshold: the signal must be accurate enough to overcome the posterior odds induced by a misleading prefix. Full closure of the gap requires nearly perfect access to the omitted latent state.","experiments":"There are no empirical experiments. The contribution is analytical. The key ingredients are a persistent latent Markov regime with retention probability rho, the textual filtration H_t, and an observer signal with fidelity gamma in [1/2, 1]. The main result is a closed-form condition under which the external signal reverses the misleading text-only posterior. This demonstrates that exact recovery of the text marginal can still produce poor situation-specific uncertainty estimates.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. still hallucinate even when the necessary evidence is present in the input? It is especially relevant for RAG over graphs/tables, tool-augmented reasoning, and mechanistic interpretability. The main contribution is a decomposition of hallucination into two internal effects: biased evidence selection and failed semantic grounding. That is more actionable than measuring hallucination only at the output level. A second practical contribution is that the proposed signals, SSR and SAS, are not only descriptive; they also support a lightweight hallucination detector without model changes or fine-tuning.","technical_approach":"$55","experiments":"The experimental emphasis is on mechanistic correlation and detection utility rather than leaderboard performance. SSR appears more sensitive to task complexity and input format, while SAS is described as a more stable indicator of hallucination risk across settings. A detector built from SSR and SAS outperforms confidence-based and consistency-based baselines, while remaining plug-and-play and requiring no model modification. As steered models whose residual streams are perturbed with activations from another model at inference, they lack validated uncertainty quantification (UQ) pipelines, even though downstream use cases including alignment auditing, deception detection, and hidden objective extraction require calibrated confidence signals to support actionable decisions.","importance":"This work presents the first UQ benchmark for activation oracles, validates the performance of common LLM confidence estimation methods under the steered setting, replicates the verbalized confidence gap previously observed in standard LLMs, and provides a practical recipe for calibrated UQ method selection. It offers practical reference for engineering deployment of interpretability tools for pretrained models.","technical_approach":"Six confidence estimation methods are benchmarked: three adapted from standard LLM UQ baselines, including joint token log-probability of predicted answers, temperature-bootstrap mode frequency, and free-form numeric self-report; three designed specifically for steered activation oracles, including MCMC power-sampling acceptance ratio, cross-chain agreement of multiple MCMC runs, and decoding stability across steering coefficient sweeps. All methods are tested on the secret-word taboo task and evaluated with calibration and ranking metrics including ECE, Brier score, and AUROC.","experiments":"Experiments are run on two activation oracles: a released Qwen3-8B oracle and a newly trained Qwen3.6-27B oracle, with 6,000 samples per configuration per model. Temperature-bootstrap mode frequency achieves the best calibration: it reaches 5.7% ECE on Qwen3-8B at sampling temperature T=1.0, far outperforming the 25.5% ECE of the log-probability baseline; on Qwen3.6-27B it reaches 10.3% ECE at T=1.5, outperforming the 13.1% ECE of the log-probability baseline. Free-form numeric self-report performs worst: on the 27B oracle, average confidence in wrong answers (98.9%) is higher than in correct answers (97.6%). For pretraining research, the distinction matters because learned representations and post-training or decoding behavior may diverge. However, we reveal a counterintuitive phenomenon: correctness does not guarantee exemplar utility, and some correct demonstrations can even reduce ICL accuracy. To study this correctness-utility gap, we introduce task-preserving perturbations, where only the exemplar input is changed, while the example remains a correct instance of the same task. However, the theoretical understanding of its mechanisms remains limited. In this work, we take a step toward bridging this gap by investigating Spectral Descent (SD), a simplified variant of Muon, together with its truncated counterpart, Truncate","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-26T13:02:49+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_26977.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","math.OC"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作研究 Muon 类优化器在非光滑目标上的收敛性。已有 Muon 理论多依赖光滑性假设，但 ReLU 网络、LAD 损失、线性规划、鲁棒矩阵恢复等场景都包含非光滑结构。论文选择 Spectral Descent（SD）及其截断版本 TSD 作为简化对象，分析正交化梯度方向在非光滑凸优化中的收敛条件。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在优化器理解，而不是直接给出新的 LLM 训练 recipe。Muon 在大模型训练中受到关注，核心原因是它对矩阵参数的梯度做近似正交化，常被视为一种矩阵几何上的预条件。论文把分析从光滑目标推进到非光滑凸目标，说明 Muon 类更新的优势并不完全依赖传统光滑性。局限也明确：理论设置仍是凸性、Lipschitz 连续性、sharpness 等结构化假设，距离真实 Transformer 预训练还有明显间隔。","technical_approach":"方法上，论文把 Muon 的核心机制抽象为 Spectral Descent：对矩阵梯度或次梯度做奇异值分解，用谱范数几何下的最陡下降方向更新参数。TSD 只保留前 s 个奇异方向，对应 Ky Fan s-范数几何，计算量低于完整 SVD。主要理论结果包括：在凸、Lipschitz、sharpness 条件下，SD 可获得全局线性收敛，收缩率受次梯度 rank 控制；TSD 在相同结构假设下也可线性收敛，并且截断在某些最坏情形下放松条件参数要求。带 decoupled weight decay 的版本被解释为与 Conditional Subgradient / Frank-Wolfe 类方法存在结构联系。为处理非光滑轨迹不稳定，作者引入邻域式次梯度选择，相当于一种空间平滑机制，并给出正则化 SD/TSD 的次线性收敛保证。","experiments":"实验用于验证理论现象，主要不是大模型预训练实验。论文报告 SD 和 Muon 在非光滑 ReLU 网络训练中比 GD 和 Adam 损失下降更快，并且对学习率 schedule 更稳健；在线性规划和矩阵分类任务上验证 SD/TSD 能有效优化非光滑目标并恢复解；在鲁棒低秩矩阵恢复的 LAD 设定中测试 RTSD-WD，观察到与理论一致的次线性收敛及对混合噪声的鲁棒性。一个实践相关点是，TSD 在 s=1 时可避免完整 SVD 的 O(n^3) 开销，但这更适合低秩矩阵问题，不能直接等同于 LLM 训练中的系统收益。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25 到 40 分钟。先看 Introduction 的 Table 1，了解它和已有 Muon 理论工作的差异；再看 Theorem 4.1 和 4.2，重点理解 rank-dependent contraction 与 Ky Fan s-norm 的作用；带 weight decay 的 Theorem 4.3 和 4.4 可快速扫过，关注 decoupled weight decay 为什么会连接到 Frank-Wolfe 式更新。实验部分建议只看 ReLU 网络和 TSD 截断消融，判断这些现象是否可能迁移到大模型优化。若目标是 LLM pretrain recipe，不需要深读证明细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies the convergence of Muon-style optimizers on non-smooth objectives. Existing theory for Muon largely assumes smoothness, while many relevant losses and model classes, such as ReLU networks, LAD formulations, linear programming objectives, and robust matrix recovery, are non-smooth. The authors analyze Spectral Descent and Truncated Spectral Descent as simplified proxies for Muon.","importance":"The main value for pretraining research is optimizer understanding, not a ready-to-use LLM training recipe. Muon is interesting because it uses matrix-wise geometry and approximate gradient orthogonalization, which can be viewed as a structured preconditioner. This work suggests that part of the benefit of this update family can be justified beyond smooth optimization. The limitation is also clear: the theory relies on convexity, Lipschitz continuity, sharpness, and related structural assumptions, so the connection to large-scale Transformer pretraining remains indirect.","technical_approach":"The paper abstracts Muon into Spectral Descent, where a matrix subgradient is converted into a steepest descent direction under spectral-norm geometry. The truncated variant keeps only the top s singular directions and corresponds to steepest descent under the Ky Fan s-norm. The main results show global linear convergence for SD under convexity, Lipschitz continuity, and sharpness, with a contraction rate governed by the subgradient rank. TSD obtains a similar linear convergence guarantee under the same structural assumptions, while reducing the SVD cost and relaxing some worst-case condition requirements. For decoupled weight decay, the authors relate regularized spectral updates to Conditional Subgradient or Frank-Wolfe-type methods, and introduce a neighborhood-based subgradient selection rule as a form of spatial smoothing for non-smooth trajectories.","experiments":"The experiments are designed to validate theory rather than to benchmark LLM pretraining. The paper reports faster empirical loss decay for SD and Muon than GD and Adam on non-smooth ReLU network training, with better robustness to learning-rate schedules. It also evaluates SD and TSD on linear programming and matrix classification tasks, showing effective minimization of non-smooth objectives and recovery of optimal solutions. For robust low-rank matrix recovery with an LAD formulation, Regularized Truncated Spectral Descent with Weight Decay follows the predicted sublinear behavior and remains robust under mixed noise. The practical note is that TSD with s=1 avoids the full O(n^3) SVD cost in matrix recovery, though this does not directly translate into an LLM systems result.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25 to 40 minutes. context, not just point estimates. The setting is Gaussian process regression. The main questions are how a transformer can approximate the predictive mean, predictive variance, and binned probability distribution, and how the approximation depends on attention depth, discretization resolution, and context length.","importance":"The contribution is mostly theoretical, but it is relevant to foundation-model pretraining research through the lens of in-context learning and synthetic prior pretraining. It gives a constructive explanation of distributional ICL: attention can behave like an iterative solver, while the output head turns estimated moments into a discretized predictive distribution. This is useful for understanding PFN-style models, uncertainty-aware ICL, and extrapolation to context sizes larger than those seen during pretraining. It does not provide a direct LLM training recipe, but it clarifies mechanisms that may transfer to broader studies of ICL and calibration.","technical_approach":"The paper constructs a transformer that approximates the GP posterior predictive distribution. Self-attention implements an iterative procedure for computing the predictive mean and variance, analogous to solving the kernel-regression quantities needed for GP prediction. A shallow MLP then maps these moments to probabilities over output bins. The approximation error is decomposed into attention-solver error, binning error, and tail truncation error. Under the stated conditions, the attention component inherits an exponential convergence rate in the number of layers. Once binning and tails are controlled, the dominant error comes from the attention block. The paper also analyzes n-generalization. Using spectral properties of the attention score matrix, it argues that attention normalization is necessary for reliable extrapolation to larger context sizes; without normalization, the effective operator changes scale as n changes, which harms both mean prediction and uncertainty estimation.","experiments":"The empirical component is controlled and mainly supports the theory in Gaussian process regression. The paper illustrates that PFN-style transformers can match posterior predictive quantiles, including lower 5% and upper 95% intervals, and studies how approximation quality changes with attention depth, bin resolution, and normalization. The central findings are that deeper attention improves the iterative approximation, bin resolution bounds the final distributional accuracy, and normalization is important for context-size generalization. The provided material does not contain enough confirmed numeric tables to report exact gains safely.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30 minutes, or read the theorems carefully if the goal is ICL theory. Then read Theorem 3.1 for the depth-error relationship and Theorem 4.1 for the full PPD approximation bound. The section on n-generalization is the most relevant part for foundation-model researchers, especially the role of normalization and the spectrum of the attention score matrix. problem，说明解释工作也可以有可迁移策略，而不必每个任务重做一套 pipeline。第二，文中设计的 contrastive reward 很有意思：用目标任务损伤减去自然文本上的一般语言建模损伤，试图分离 task-specific heads 和 general-purpose heads。这个想法对训练信号设计有参考价值，尤其是在想从“有害但不专属”的因果因素里剥离出“真正任务相关”的因素时。若从 LLM 基座研究角度看，它更像是 mechanistic interpretability 的方法论文，而不是 pretrain recipe 论文。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者把 GPT-2 small 的 144 个 attention heads 视为离散动作空间。每一步动作就是选择一个 head，在 hook_z 位置把该 head 输出置零，观察该消融对任务指标和控制指标的影响。环境是一个标准 Gymnasium MDP，episode 最多 50 步，且同一 episode 内不能重复选择同一 head。训练任务只有两个：induction 和 IOI。每次 reset 时随机采样任务并重新生成 batch，避免策略记住固定 head 排名。奖励设计是全文最关键的部分。对目标任务，使用 ablation 前后 logit difference 的下降作为损伤；对控制任务，使用自然英文文本上的 cross-entropy 上升作为一般语言建模损伤；最终 reward 是两者的差。这样，若某个 head 对所有任务都重要，消融它会同时伤害目标任务和一般文本，净奖励不会太高；若某个 head 主要服务于当前任务，reward 才会显著为正。观测包括三部分：当前任务 one-hot、已尝试 head 的 mask、已尝试 head 的归一化 reward 向量。后两者让策略能在单个 episode 内根据已获得反馈调整后续搜索。策略网络是一个小型 actor-critic MLP，输入 290 维，经过两层 256 隐层，输出 144 维动作分布和价值头。训练用 PPO，8 个并行环境，共 2e5 环境步。作者还加了一个 best-of-K 决策包装：策略先采样 K 个候选动作，环境并行评估后选 reward 最高者执行。K=1 是普通 PPO，K=5 则相当于给策略加了轻量规划/重采样。泛化测试使用训练中未见过的 docstring completion 任务，检查策略是否学到了可迁移的结构先验，而不是只记住 induction/IOI 的局部模式。","experiments":"实验核心结论是：一个在 induction 和 IOI 上训练的 PPO 策略，能够在训练任务上接近每个 episode 的 oracle 选头表现，并在未见过的 docstring completion 上也有不错的迁移。摘要提到其偏好的 heads 与已有人工或半自动分析得到的已知电路有重合，这说明策略学到的不是纯噪声启发式。全文给出的关键设置包括：模型是 GPT-2 small，动作空间 144，episode 长度 50，8 个并行环境，训练总步数 2×10^5；best-of-K 评估报告 K∈{1,5}。从方法设计看，最重要的实验应包括四类：一是训练曲线，比较单环境 PPO 崩塌与多环境 PPO 稳定训练；二是 reward 设计消融，验证若去掉 control cross-entropy 项，策略会偏向通用脆弱头而不是任务电路；三是与 oracle、随机、可能还有简单贪心基线的比较；四是 held-out docstring 迁移。提供的全文片段没有完整结果表和具体百分比，因此不应虚构数值。但可以明确的是，作者把“泛化到未见任务”作为主要卖点，而不是只在单任务上复现已知头部。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读方法和实验，不必深究 RL 细节。先看 Introduction，明确它要替代的是 ACDC、EAP 这类单任务电路发现流程。然后直接看 Method 里的 reward 定义，这是整篇最有信息量的地方；如果 reward 不成立，后面的 PPO 只是实现细节。接着看 observation 设计和 best-of-K 包装，理解作者为什么把问题做成 sequential search，而不是一次性给 144 个头打分。实验部分优先看三类图表：reward 消融、训练任务与 held-out 任务上的 oracle gap、以及被选 heads 与已知 induction/IOI 电路的重合情况。若只想判断是否值得继续跟，可以用 20-30 分钟读完方法和主要结果。值得深挖的点有两个：一是 contrastive reward 是否真的隔离了 task-specific computation，还是只是偏好“对控制集伤害较小”的头；二是动作空间目前只覆盖 attention heads，若扩展到 MLP、residual stream 或 path-level intervention，RL 是否仍然可扩展。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether circuit discovery in mechanistic interpretability can be turned from a bespoke, task-by-task analysis pipeline into a learnable reinforcement-learning problem. Concretely, the goal is to train an agent that interacts with a frozen GPT-2 small by ablating attention heads one at a time and identifying heads that are causally important for a target behavior while being relatively unimportant for general language modeling. The hard part is not ablation itself, but reward design: the agent must discover task-specific circuitry rather than simply attack globally fragile heads.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, so skim is the right level. Still, it has two useful ideas. First, it reframes circuit discovery as a sequential decision problem, suggesting that interpretability procedures may admit transferable policies rather than requiring a fresh handcrafted pipeline for every task. Second, the contrastive reward is the main contribution: damage on the target task is offset by damage on a natural-text control objective, which is an attempt to separate task-specific heads from general-purpose heads. That reward construction is relevant beyond interpretability because it is a concrete example of how to isolate a specialized causal signal from a broader capability signal.","technical_approach":"$57","experiments":"$58","reading_guide":"This is best read as a methods paper with a focused empirical demonstration. After that, read the observation design and the best-of-K decision wrapper to understand why the authors formulate the problem as sequential search rather than one-shot head scoring. In the experiments, prioritize reward ablations, the gap to oracle on train and held-out tasks, and the overlap between selected heads and known induction/IOI circuits. A 20-30 minute pass is enough to decide whether the idea is worth following. 开发中的数据选择、反馈偏差、路由偏差、评测偏差，统一放进因果推断视角下讨论。对于做大规模训练的人，这种视角的价值在于减少错误归因，避免把 confounding 当成 recipe 经验。","technical_approach":"这是一篇立场和框架型论文，不是提出单一算法。技术内容主要分三层。\n\n第一层是问题形式化。作者用潜在结果框架把 LLM 开发中的系统选择写成 treatment A，环境或上下文写成 X，结果写成 Y。这里的 A 可以是预训练数据混合权重、prompt 模板、router 决策、retrieval policy、judge 配置、对齐策略等；Y 可以是 perplexity、任务成功率、偏好分数、安全指标、延迟或综合 utility。这样一来，工程上常问的“如果换一种配置会怎样”就变成估计 E[Y(a)] 或 treatment effect 的问题。\n\n第二层是场景映射。全文把因果方法放到了 LLM pipeline 的多个位置：pretraining 数据决策、alignment 与 preference data、deployment 中的 routing、evaluation 中的 arena 和 LLM-as-a-judge、以及 agentic workflow。作者反复强调一个共同点：这些环节大量依赖历史日志，而日志不是随机采样得到的，常常带有 selection bias、policy-induced bias、label bias 和 distribution shift。比如大模型路由系统会把更难的问题分给更大的模型，若直接比较日志结果，可能错误得出“大模型更差”的结论。\n\n第三层是方法原则。文章讨论了在 LLM 场景中应用因果推断时需要满足或近似满足的条件，如可识别性、混杂控制、重叠性、稳定性，以及如何结合现代因果机器学习工具处理高维特征、偏差反馈和有限标注预算。虽然全文更偏综述和议程设置，但它并不是泛泛而谈，而是在每个环节都指出了为什么 observational logs 不够、随机实验为什么贵、因果估计能补什么缺口。\n\n从预训练角度看，最有用的是它把“数据 mixture 选择”明确当成 intervention 问题，而不是经验调参问题。这个表述很重要，因为它自然引出反事实问题：如果不加入某个域，或者把某域权重从 5% 改到 15%，最终能力变化到底来自数据本身，还是来自与 tokenizer、训练步数、评测集重叠、课程顺序等因素的耦合。","experiments":"这篇文章的核心不是实验突破，而是框架梳理与案例化论证。全文没有像方法论文那样给出统一 benchmark 会怎样失真，以及因果方法在哪些地方能提供更稳健的估计。\n\n从内容结构看，作者主要给出的是问题实例、偏差来源和可行研究方向，而不是一个完整的实证系统。因此阅读时不要期待新的训练曲线或大模型性能表。更应该关注的是它如何拆解偏差来源：例如路由导致的样本难度偏差、人工偏好评测中的风格偏差、arena 或 judge 中因展示顺序和回答长度带来的系统性偏差、以及 agent in pass@K code generation: with a fixed budget of K attempts, iid sampling often produces near-duplicate solutions that follow the same algorithmic path.","importance":"The contribution is in post-training and test-time compute rather than base pretraining. Its relevance is that it changes the policy class for repeated sampling. Instead of drawing K answers independently from one distribution, the model is trained to produce a coordinated set of complementary strategies and then solve once per strategy. This is useful for thinking about verifier-driven training, credit assignment under pass@K objectives, and data generation for reasoning models where diversity at the strategy level matters more than surface-form variation.","technical_approach":"CPPO reframes pass@K as joint exploration over strategies. A planner autoregressively emits a tuple of K=4 high-level methods, where each method is conditioned on the previous ones. A shared solver then generates one solution for each method. Credit is assigned at two levels. Solver tokens receive branch-level verifier feedback for each generated solution. Planner tokens receive a tuple-level outcome reward that reflects whether at least one branch succeeds. Because tuple-level rewards are sparse and can be exploited by malformed, duplicated, or answer-leaking plans, the planner reward is gated by a narrow plan-validity model Jψ, giving R_plan = Jψ · R_out. The model is optimized with split-region GRPO updates over planner and solver token regions.","experiments":"The experiments cover APPS, CodeContests, and LiveCodeBench-v6 under a fixed K=4 budget. CPPO improves over the strongest non-CPPO baseline in all nine reported size-benchmark cells, with six cells significant under paired bootstrap at p<0.05. Compared with Tuple Planner SFT, pass@4 gains range from +0.08 to +0.30, indicating that planner initialization alone does not explain the result. However, we identify that agentic RL training induces increasing redundant tool calls and blurs the model's intrinsic knowledge boundary, where the model fails to distinguish when tools are needed versus when parametric knowledge suffices. Existing solutions based on reward shaping create coarse-grained optimization targets that tend to incentivize indiscriminate tool-call ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-26T12:42:23+00:00","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2605_26952.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 agentic RL 里一个很具体但常被忽略的问题：模型在有工具可用时，随着 RL 训练推进，会越来越依赖外部工具，出现不必要的调用，甚至把本来可以靠参数知识直接回答的问题也交给工具。作者把这个现象表述为 intrinsic knowledge boundary 被模糊：模型不再清楚哪些问题在自身知识边界内，哪些问题确实需要外部检索或执行。已有方法多用 reward shaping 直接惩罚工具调用次数，但这种做法把“少用工具”当成粗粒度目标，容易诱导模型为了拿奖励而机械压低调用次数，结果是 reward hacking，且无法针对单个样本判断某次调用到底是必要还是冗余。","importance":"对预训练本身的直接贡献不大，它更接近 post-training / agentic RL 的训练信号设计；但对理解“参数知识与外部工具如何分工”有启发。价值主要有三点。第一，它把工具使用效率问题从 reward engineering 转成了 instance-level boundary probing，这个视角比简单惩罚 tool call 更干净。第二，它说明 agentic RL 会系统性改变模型的行为边界，而不是只提升任务成功率，这对后续研究 pretrain 与 tool-use 接口、以及 parametric memory 与 retrieval 的协同有参考意义。第三，它给出一个较实用的 on-policy 插件方案，不改主 RL 目标，只加辅助监督，适合接到现有 GRPO 类流程里。量级上属于中等增量，不是范式级变化，但问题定义是清楚的。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 AKBE。做法不是改 reward，而是在每个训练 batch 上显式探测知识边界。具体来说，对同一个问题做 dual-path rollouts：一条路径允许调用工具，另一条路径禁止工具，只靠模型自身作答。然后根据两条路径的正确性和工具使用情况，把样本划分为几类，并构造不同的监督信号。\n\n如果 with-tool 正确、no-tool 错误，说明问题超出当前参数知识边界，属于 Tool-dependent；这时不只是鼓励“会用工具”，还要在正确轨迹里选最少工具调用的那条，强化最省调用的正确模式。\n\n如果 with-tool 正确、no-tool 也正确，说明工具并非必要，属于 Efficiency；这时用 no-tool 正确轨迹做监督，压掉冗余调用。\n\n如果 with-tool 错误、no-tool 正确，说明工具引入了噪声或干扰，属于 Hallucination；这时同样强化 no-tool 正确轨迹，目的是减少有害依赖。\n\n如果两条都错，则 Both-wrong，不额外加边界监督，只保留原始 RL 更新。\n\n关键点有两个。第一，监督信号是 on-policy 的，因为 dual-path rollout 来自当前策略，知识边界会随训练动态变化，方法可以同步跟踪，而不是依赖静态标注。第二，它不直接把工具次数写进 reward，因此避免了 reward 与任务正确率之间的硬耦合。训练上，AKBE 作为 auxiliary loss 与标准 agentic RL 目标联合优化，作者强调它是 plug-and-play，可接到不同 agentic RL 算法上。\n\n和已有 reward shaping 方法相比，区别不在于“也想减少工具调用”，而在于它试图回答更细的问题：当前这个样本到底需不需要工具，若需要，最小必要调用是多少。这个 per-instance 判别是方法成立的基础。","experiments":"文中在 7 个 QA benchmark、2 个 backbone 上做了实验，并与标准 agentic RL 及若干效率优化基线比较。主结果是：平均任务准确率提升 1.85 个点，工具调用数下降 18%，tool productivity 提升 25%。作者强调这不是靠牺牲准确率换效率，而是在多数设置下同时改善两者。\n\n实验设计里比较重要的部分不是单一均值，而是几类分析。其一，兼容性实验：AKBE 可以作为模块接到不同 agentic RL 算法上，而不是只对某个特定 trainer 有效。其二，训练动态分析：随着训练进行，样本在 Tool-dependent / Efficiency / Hallucination 等类别中的分布会变化，说明知识边界不是静态属性，而是会被 RL 推动迁移。其三，类别级作用分析：不同监督信号分别对应不同失败模式，Efficiency 主要压冗余调用，Hallucination 主要处理工具带来的错误覆盖，Tool-dependent 则帮助模型学到“该用工具时怎么更省地用”。\n\n从摘要和正文可见的数字看，论文更强调平均趋势和行为改善，没有给出特别大规模的模型或数据设置，重点是训练机制而非规模扩展。若从严一点看，18% 的调用下降和 1.85 的准确率提升是不错的工程结果，但还不足以说明它改变了 agentic RL 的基本 scaling 规律。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，抓住问题定义：RL 训练会让工具调用持续上升，且简单 reward shaping 会带来 reward hacking。然后重点看方法部分里 dual-path rollout 和四类样本划分，这是整篇最关键的设计。若时间有限，优先看定义 knowledge boundary 的段落、分类规则，以及 auxiliary loss 如何并入原有 RL 目标。\n\n实验部分建议看三类图表：主结果表，证明准确率和效率能同时改善；训练动态图，理解 knowledge boundary 会随训练移动；消融或类别分析，确认四类监督信号各自的作用。如果只打算花 20 到 30 分钟，读完方法主图、主结果表和一组动态分析基本就够了。\n\n值得深挖的点有两个。第一，dual-path rollout 的额外采样成本是否会抵消节省下来的工具成本，正文里如果有 wall-clock 或 sample efficiency 分析要仔细看。第二，knowledge boundary 的判定依赖当前策略采样，可能受 rollout 方差影响，尤其在 no-tool 路径本身不稳定时，分类噪声会不会误导监督，这是判断方法上限的关键。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a specific failure mode in agentic RL: once tools are available, RL training tends to push the model toward excessive tool use. The model increasingly calls tools even when its parametric knowledge is sufficient, or uses more calls than necessary. The authors frame this as a blurred intrinsic knowledge boundary: the policy no longer cleanly distinguishes when to rely on internal knowledge versus when external tools are actually needed. Prior work mostly addresses this with reward shaping that penalizes tool usage, but that creates a coarse optimization target. It encourages global suppression of tool calls, invites reward hacking, and cannot decide at the per-instance level whether a given call is necessary or redundant.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense; it sits in post-training and agentic RL. Still, it is relevant to foundation-model research because it makes the parametric-knowledge-versus-tool-use interface explicit. The main contribution is conceptual and practical rather than paradigm-shifting. Conceptually, it replaces reward-level heuristics with instance-level boundary probing. Practically, it offers an on-policy auxiliary training signal that can be attached to existing GRPO-style pipelines without rewriting the main RL objective. For researchers interested in how RL reshapes knowledge usage, retrieval reliance, and tool behavior, the paper is useful. The impact is moderate: the problem formulation is sharp, the method is sensible, but it does not yet change the broader training recipe for LLMs.","technical_approach":"$5b","experiments":"$5c","reading_guide":"$5d","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"把“该不该用工具”从粗糙 reward 里拆成可观测的双路径对照信号，这个训练信号设计值得抄；但先核对实验是否覆盖多工具/长轨迹，别被单一任务结论误导。","en":"Worth skimming for the dual-path rollout signal that turns “tool or not” into an observable contrastive supervision; validate it on multi-tool, long-horizon setups before trusting the claim beyond a narrow benchmark."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Tencent Inc","The Chinese University of Hong Kong (Shenzhen)","MSU-BIT University","The Chinese University of Hong Kong ♡Shenzhen MSU-BIT University"],"authors":["Dingwei Chen","Zefang Zong","Zhipeng Ma","Leo Luo","Yang Li","Chengming Li","Peng Chen","Jie Jiang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["agentic-rl","tool-use","reward-hacking","on-policy","training-signals"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26952","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.26952","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.26731","title":"It's Not the Capability: Harness Sensitivity Is Non-Monotone Across LLM Agent Tiers","abstract":"A prevalent assumption in LLM agent deployment holds that more structured harnesses universally improve   reliability, and that higher-capability models need proportionally less structural guidance -- together   implying a monotone inverse relationship between model capability tier and optimal harness complexity. We   test this hypothesis through a controlled 432-run experiment crossing six models across four capability   tiers with three harness conditions (light, balanced, strict) on HEAT-24, ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-26T09:08:41+00:00","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2605_26731.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的是 agent harness 设计，而不是预训练本身。它要检验一个在实际部署里很常见、但很少被系统验证的假设：更复杂、更严格的 harness 是否总能提升 agent 可靠性；如果不是，不同能力层级的模型对 harness 复杂度的敏感性是否至少呈单调关系。作者把这个问题做成了一个受控实验：固定任务、固定 workspace、固定 verifier，只改变 harness 复杂度和模型类型，观察成功率与失败模式如何变化。核心结论是否定的：harness 敏感性既不单调，也不能简单用参数规模或常规“能力层级”来预测。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的直接相关性不高，但对 post-train、agentization 和模型使用接口有一点方法论启发。它提醒人不要把 agent 失败轻易归因于模型能力不足，很多失败来自系统提示结构与模型 instruction-following 风格之间的错配。这个观察对评估 reasoning model、chat model 在工具环境中的真实表现有帮助，也对构造 agent SFT / RL 数据有一点启发：如果 harness 本身改变了错误分布，那么用某一种固定 harness 采集的数据，可能只是在教模型适应特定交互协议，而不是提升通用任务能力。论文里另一个有用的点是，2B 模型在 harness 稳定性上可以接近更强开源模型，作者据此认为 instruction-tuning quality 比参数量更能解释 harness sensitivity。这个判断虽然来自小规模实验，但方向上是合理的。","technical_approach":"方法很直接，也比较干净。作者构建了 HEAT-24，一个 deterministic 的合成 benchmark，包含 24 个任务、6 个类别，所有任务都在同一个 git workspace 上完成。workspace 里有 12 个文件，包括 YAML/JSON 配置、Python 代码与测试、Markdown 文档、CSV 数据和 changelog 片段。每次运行前重置仓库，模型通过输出带有 <<<WRITE:path>>> ... <<<END>>> 标记的内容来修改文件，runner 再把修改应用到 workspace，并用 git diff、解析器和测试脚本做自动验证。这样设计的好处是把 agent 评测从开放式自然语言 judge 拉回到可重复、二值、可审计的环境。\n\nharness 被分成三档。light 只有角色说明和原始任务；balanced 增加四步流程模板和允许修改的文件列表；strict 再加入六阶段流程、显式成功标准、验证规范和恢复步骤。注意一个关键控制变量：文件修改 marker 在所有条件下都出现在原始任务指令里，因此三种 harness 的差异主要来自额外过程约束，而不是是否提供写文件协议。作者用 6 个模型、4 个 capability tier、3 种 harness 条件，共 432 次运行。模型既有 frontier chat，也有 frontier reasoning，还有开源和小模型。评估指标是 TSR/VTSR，在这个 benchmark 里两者相同，因为 verifier 没有基础设施失败。作者还定义了 6 类自动失败标签：format_violation、wrong_answer、wrong_file、missing_change、unrelated_change、tests_still_fail，用来分析 harness 到底改变了什么。\n\n从方法角度看，这篇工作的价值不在新算法，而在实验控制。它把“prompt engineering 是否有效”这种容易陷入经验主义的问题，转成了一个可复现实验：同一任务、同一 verifier、同一输出协议，只改变 harness 结构。","experiments":"最醒目的结果是 Gemini 2.5 Flash 在更复杂 harness 下显著变差。light harness 的 VTSR 为 95.8%，balanced 降到 58.3%，strict 为 66.7%，分别下降 37.5 和 29.2 个百分点。失败主因不是任务理解错误，而是 format_violation：balanced 条件下 10 个失败里 10 个是格式问题，strict 下 8 个失败里 8 个也是格式问题。这说明对某些强 chat 模型，过度结构化的 harness 会把模型推向“过度遵守流程文本、却偏离实际执行协议”的状态。\n\n作者还报告了相反方向的现象：frontier reasoning 模型从 strict harness 中获益最多，因此整体模式不是“模型越强越不需要结构”，而是 chat-style 与 reasoning-style 模型对结构化约束的响应不同。另一个值得记住的点是，参数量不是好代理变量。文中提到一个 2B 模型 Gemma4:e2B 在 harness 稳定性上可以匹配 strong-open tier，这支持作者的解释：instruction tuning 质量和交互风格，比裸参数规模更决定 harness sensitivity。\n\n需要注意的是，作者自己也承认 tier-level 结论只能算 exploratory，因为每个 tier 只放了一个模型，不能把模型特性直接上升为层级规律。HEAT-24 任务也都是 synthetic、deterministic、无外部工具调用，因而结果更像“受控环境中的接口敏感性测量”，而不是对真实 agent 系统的全面外推。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不建议投入太多时间。先看 Introduction 和 HEAT-24 设计，确认 benchmark 控制了哪些变量；再看 Table 3 和 failure taxonomy，抓住作者真正发现的现象：复杂 harness 既可能帮忙，也可能制造格式性失败。最后看 discussion 里关于 parameter count 与 instruction-tuning quality 的解释。预计 15–20 The authors turn this into a controlled experiment by fixing the task environment, workspace, and verifier, and varying only harness complexity and model choice. Their answer is no on both counts.
